# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Actions/ForceDeleteNodeAction.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeForceDeleted;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNotTrashedException;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersionBlob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\StorageStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\TreeGuard;

/**
 * Permanently soft-delete a trashed node and its subtree (§2.10 stage 2). Only a
 * node already in the trash qualifies (a live node goes through the trash first)
 * and the root never does; the subtree is read under the storage tree lock. The
 * order matters and is the GC contract (§2.3 / §2.4):
 *   1. detach every version↔blob edge in the subtree — the edge IS the liveness
 *      truth (H.3), so dropping it makes the blob GC-eligible; the bytes are
 *      NEVER deleted here (M1b's two-phase GC reclaims them);
 *   2. soft-delete (deleted_at) the node rows in one bulk UPDATE;
 *   3. decrement used_bytes by the freed LIVE size (§1.5 — trash kept the bytes;
 *      forceDelete is where the quota is actually reclaimed).
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class ForceDeleteNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private StorageStoreRepository $storageStore,
        private BlobStore $blobs,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
        private TreeGuard $tree,
    ) {}

    public function execute(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): void
    {
        $storage = $node->storage;

        $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $storage, $actorId): void {
            $this->database->lockTree($node->storage_id);

            $current = $this->tree->sync($node, $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$node->id]), $node->id));

            $this->tree->assertNotRoot(node: $current, operation: 'delete');

            if ($current->trashed_at === null) {
                throw NodeNotTrashedException::forNode($current);
            }

            $subtree = $this->nodes->subtree($current);
            $ids = $subtree->modelKeys();
            $freedBytes = 0;

            foreach ($subtree as $member) {
                if ($member->kind !== NodeKind::File) {
                    continue;
                }

                $freedBytes += $this->detachFilePayload($member);
            }

            $this->store->softDeleteSubtree(collect($ids));

            if ($freedBytes > 0) {
                $this->storageStore->adjustUsedBytes(storage: $storage, delta: -$freedBytes);
            }

            event(new NodeForceDeleted(
                storageId: $current->storage_id,
                nodeIds: array_values($ids),
                freedBytes: $freedBytes,
                actorId: $actorId,
            ));
        });
    }

    /**
     * Detach every blob edge of every version of a file node and return its live
     * head size (the bytes the storage stops accounting for). Detaching only drops
     * the reference — the physical object is reclaimed later by GC.
     */
    private function detachFilePayload(Node $node): int
    {
        /** @var File|null $file */
        $file = $node->file()->first();

        if (!$file instanceof File) {
            return 0;
        }

        foreach ($file->versions()->get() as $version) {
            $this->detachVersionEdges($version);
        }

        return $node->size_bytes;
    }

    private function detachVersionEdges(FileVersion $version): void
    {
        foreach ($version->blobs as $blob) {
            /** @var FileVersionBlob $pivot */
            $pivot = $blob->getRelationValue('pivot');

            $this->blobs->detach(
                version: $version,
                blob: $blob,
                role: $pivot->role,
                ordinal: $pivot->ordinal,
            );
        }
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Actions/MoveNodeAction.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\MoveNodeData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeMoved;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\CrossStorageMoveException;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\CycleException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;
use Vaulter\Support\TreeGuard;

/**
 * Re-parent a node within its storage tree — the §2.2 / H.4 single parent_id
 * UPDATE. Every guard runs inside the transaction, after the storage tree lock and
 * the row locks on the node and its destination:
 *   1. the root never moves;
 *   2. the destination is a live folder of the same storage (no destination = root);
 *   3. cycle — the destination must not be the node itself or one of its own
 *      descendants (checked via the bounded subtree, NOT whereHas('descendants'));
 *   4. sibling-name conflict at the destination.
 *
 * The tree lock is what makes the cycle check sound: row locks on the node and the
 * destination alone do not cover the ancestor chain, so two moves over disjoint rows
 * (A into B's child, B into A's child) could both pass and close a loop.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class MoveNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
        private TreeGuard $tree,
    ) {}

    public function execute(MoveNodeData $data): Node
    {
        $node = $data->node;
        $intoId = $this->tree->destinationId(storage: $node->storage, into: $data->into);

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $intoId, $data): Node {
            $this->database->lockTree($node->storage_id);

            $locked = $this->tree->lock([$node->id, $intoId]);
            $current = $this->tree->sync($node, $this->tree->locked($locked, $node->id));
            $into = $this->tree->locked($locked, $intoId);

            $this->tree->assertNotRoot(node: $current, operation: 'move');
            if (!$this->tree->sameStorage($into, $current->storage_id)) {
                throw CrossStorageMoveException::between(fromStorageId: $current->storage_id, toStorageId: $into->storage_id);
            }

            $this->tree->assertActiveFolder($into);
            $this->assertNoCycle(node: $current, into: $into);

            $fromParentId = $current->parent_id;
            $toParentId = $into->id;

            if (Identity::stringify($fromParentId) === Identity::stringify($toParentId)) {
                return $current;
            }

            $this->assertNameAvailable(node: $current, into: $into);

            $moved = $this->store->move(node: $current, parentId: $toParentId);

            event(new NodeMoved(
                node: $moved,
                fromParentId: $fromParentId,
                toParentId: $toParentId,
                actorId: $data->actorId,
            ));

            return $moved;
        });
    }

    /**
     * The cycle guard (H.4): a node cannot move into itself nor any node in its
     * own subtree. We materialise the bounded subtree once and test membership —
     * never a recursive correlated aggregate in the request path.
     */
    private function assertNoCycle(Node $node, Node $into): void
    {
        if (Identity::stringify($into->id) === Identity::stringify($node->id)) {
            throw CycleException::forMove(nodeId: $node->id, intoId: $into->id);
        }

        $descendantIds = array_map(
            Identity::stringify(...),
            $this->nodes->subtree($node)->modelKeys(),
        );

        $this->metrics->histogram(
            name: 'vaulter_subtree_nodes_touched',
            value: count($descendantIds),
            labels: ['operation' => 'subtree_mutate'],
        );

        if (in_array(Identity::stringify($into->id), $descendantIds, strict: true)) {
            throw CycleException::forMove(nodeId: $node->id, intoId: $into->id);
        }
    }

    private function assertNameAvailable(Node $node, Node $into): void
    {
        $exists = $this->nodes->siblingNameExists(
            storage: $node->storage,
            parentId: $into->id,
            name: $node->name,
            ignoreId: $node->id,
        );

        if ($exists) {
            throw NodeNameConflictException::forName(name: $node->name, parentId: $into->id);
        }
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Actions/RenameNodeAction.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\RenameNodeData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeRenamed;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\NodeName;
use Vaulter\Support\TreeGuard;

/**
 * Rename a node in place (§2.2), subject to the sibling-name conflict check at
 * its current parent (the node itself is excluded from the check). The root is
 * never renamed; the check runs against the locked row.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class RenameNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
        private TreeGuard $tree,
    ) {}

    public function execute(RenameNodeData $data): Node
    {
        $node = $data->node;
        $name = NodeName::normalize($data->name);

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $name, $data): Node {
            $this->database->lockTreeShared($node->storage_id);
            $current = $this->tree->sync($node, $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$node->id]), $node->id));

            // The parent row is locked too, so a create into the same folder cannot claim
            // the new name between the sibling check and the UPDATE. Locking upward from
            // the node cannot cycle: the tree is acyclic and moves are excluded.
            $this->tree->lock([$current->parent_id]);
            $this->tree->assertNotRoot(node: $current, operation: 'rename');

            $fromName = $current->name;

            if ($fromName === $name) {
                return $current;
            }

            $exists = $this->nodes->siblingNameExists(
                storage: $current->storage,
                parentId: $current->parent_id,
                name: $name,
                ignoreId: $current->id,
            );

            if ($exists) {
                throw NodeNameConflictException::forName(name: $name, parentId: $current->parent_id);
            }

            $renamed = $this->store->rename(node: $current, name: $name);

            event(new NodeRenamed(
                node: $renamed,
                fromName: $fromName,
                toName: $name,
                actorId: $data->actorId,
            ));

            return $renamed;
        });
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Actions/RestoreNodeAction.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeRestored;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\InvalidDestinationException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNotTrashedException;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;
use Vaulter\Support\TreeGuard;

/**
 * Restore a trashed node and its subtree (§2.10). Clears the trashed triplet
 * across the bounded subtree in one bulk UPDATE. Runs under the storage tree lock
 * (it re-parents) with the node, its original parent and the root locked, and only
 * accepts a node that is actually in the trash. Two deterministic destination
 * rules (§2.10 restore-collision):
 *   - if the original parent is gone (deleted / itself trashed / no longer a
 *     folder), re-home the node to the storage root rather than dangling it;
 *   - if a LIVE sibling now owns the node's name at the resolved destination,
 *     throw NodeNameConflictException — the caller renames then retries.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class RestoreNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
        private TreeGuard $tree,
    ) {}

    public function execute(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {
        $rootId = $this->tree->destinationId(storage: $node->storage, into: null);

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $rootId, $actorId): Node {
            $this->database->lockTree($node->storage_id);

            $current = $this->tree->sync($node, $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$node->id, $rootId]), $node->id));

            if ($current->trashed_at === null) {
                throw NodeNotTrashedException::forNode($current);
            }

            // The original parent is read from the locked row, not the caller's model.
            // Locking it after the node is safe: the tree lock excludes every other
            // operation that holds more than one node row.
            $originalParentId = $current->trashed_from_parent_id ?? $current->parent_id;
            $locked = $this->tree->lock([$originalParentId]);
            $destinationParentId = $this->resolveDestinationParent(node: $current, locked: $locked, rootId: $rootId);

            if ($this->liveSiblingOwnsName(node: $current, parentId: $destinationParentId)) {
                throw NodeNameConflictException::forName(name: $current->name, parentId: $destinationParentId);
            }

            $ids = $this->nodes->subtree($current)->modelKeys();

            // Re-home the subtree root first (so the cleared triplet lands on a
            // node already hung at its restored destination), then clear trash.
            if (Identity::stringify($current->parent_id) !== Identity::stringify($destinationParentId)) {
                $this->store->move(node: $current, parentId: $destinationParentId);
            }

            $this->store->restoreSubtree(collect($ids));

            $fresh = $current->refresh();

            event(new NodeRestored(node: $fresh, actorId: $actorId));

            return $fresh;
        });
    }

    /**
     * The destination parent for the restored node: its recorded original parent
     * when that row is still a live folder of the same storage, else the root. The
     * row was locked with the node, so it cannot be trashed or moved meanwhile.
     *
     * @param Collection<string, Node> $locked
     */
    private function resolveDestinationParent(Node $node, Collection $locked, int|string $rootId): int|string
    {
        $originalParentId = $node->trashed_from_parent_id ?? $node->parent_id;

        if ($originalParentId === null) {
            return $rootId;
        }

        $parent = $locked->get(Identity::stringify($originalParentId));

        if (!$parent instanceof Node || !$this->tree->sameStorage($parent, $node->storage_id)) {
            return $rootId;
        }

        try {
            $this->tree->assertActiveFolder($parent);
        } catch (InvalidDestinationException) {
            return $rootId;
        }

        return $parent->id;
    }

    private function liveSiblingOwnsName(Node $node, int|string $parentId): bool
    {
        return $this->nodes->siblingNameExists(
            storage: $node->storage,
            parentId: $parentId,
            name: $node->name,
            ignoreId: $node->id,
        );
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Actions/TrashNodeAction.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeTrashed;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\InvalidDestinationException;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\TreeGuard;

/**
 * Soft-trash a node and its whole subtree atomically (§2.10 / D17) under the storage
 * tree lock. The root is never trashed and a node already in the trash is rejected. The trashed
 * triplet (trashed_at, trashed_by, and trashed_from_parent_id on the root) is
 * stamped across the bounded subtree in one bulk UPDATE; the sibling-unique index
 * excludes trashed rows so the name frees up for a new sibling.
 *
 * used_bytes is NOT decremented — trashed bytes still occupy storage until
 * forceDelete (§1.5: quota counts what is physically retained, and a restore must
 * bring the bytes back). The decrement happens only in ForceDeleteNodeAction.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class TrashNodeAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private NodeStoreRepository $store,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
        private TreeGuard $tree,
    ) {}

    public function execute(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {
        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($node, $actorId): Node {
            // The subtree UPDATE locks every descendant row after the node row, in no
            // particular id order; the tree lock keeps that from crossing a concurrent
            // move or restore that holds a descendant and waits for an ancestor.
            $this->database->lockTree($node->storage_id);

            $current = $this->tree->sync($node, $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$node->id]), $node->id));

            $this->tree->assertNotRoot(node: $current, operation: 'trash');

            if ($current->trashed_at !== null) {
                throw InvalidDestinationException::trashed($current);
            }

            $ids = $this->nodes->subtree($current)->modelKeys();

            $this->store->trashSubtree(
                ids: collect($ids),
                root: $current,
                actorId: $actorId,
                at: now(),
            );

            $fresh = $current->refresh();

            event(new NodeTrashed(node: $fresh, actorId: $actorId));

            return $fresh;
        });
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Actions/UploadFileAction.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Actions;

use LogicException;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\UploadFileData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\FileUploaded;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeVersionCommitted;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\CrossStorageMoveException;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\QuotaExceededException;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\StorageStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\LoadTier;
use Vaulter\Support\NodeName;
use Vaulter\Support\TreeGuard;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Commit an upload into the Drive (§2.4 commit flow): stage → quota → BlobStore.commitStaged
 * (content-addressed dedup) → File + FileVersion(version_no) + version↔blob edge
 * → file node (current_version_id, mime, size, extension).
 *
 * Quota (§1.5 eager): the incoming size is checked against the storage's
 * quota_bytes by a conditional UPDATE WITHIN the transaction, before CAS promotion.
 * Preparation happens once outside SQL so transaction retries reuse the same bytes.
 *
 * Two paths share one Action:
 *   - new file → create Node(kind=file) + File + FileVersion(version_no=1);
 *   - new version (upload onto an existing file node) → bump version_no, repoint
 *     current_version_id, mark the prior head superseded, and adjust used_bytes by
 *     the SIZE DELTA (new size − previous head size), not the full size.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced upstream at the HTTP controller
 * boundary (NodeController + AccessResolver); this Action stays pure.
 */
final readonly class UploadFileAction
{
    public function __construct(
        private BlobStore $blobs,
        private ContentStore $content,
        private FileStoreRepository $files,
        private FileVersionStoreRepository $versions,
        private NodeStoreRepository $nodeStore,
        private NodeReadRepository $nodeRead,
        private StorageStoreRepository $storageStore,
        private MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
        private TreeGuard $tree,
    ) {}

    public function execute(UploadFileData $data): Node
    {
        return $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_upload_chunk_duration_seconds',
            operation: fn (): Node => $this->executeUpload($data),
            labels: [
                'protocol' => 'single',
                'tier' => LoadTier::label(),
            ],
        );
    }

    private function executeUpload(UploadFileData $data): Node
    {
        $name = $data->isNewVersion() ? null : NodeName::normalize($data->resolvedName());
        $parentId = $data->isNewVersion() ? null : $this->tree->destinationId(storage: $data->storage, into: $data->into);
        $staged = $this->content->stage($data->stream);

        try {
            // Revision deltas must use the locked head; a stale caller cannot reject them.
            if (!$data->isNewVersion()) {
                $this->assertWithinQuota(data: $data, delta: $staged->sizeBytes());
            }

            $node = $this->database->transaction(function () use ($data, $staged, $name, $parentId): Node {
                // Lock order: drive → nodes → file → quota → CAS, so concurrent writers never deadlock.
                $this->database->lockTreeShared($data->storage->id);

                if ($name !== null && $parentId !== null) {
                    $this->assertDestination(data: $data, parentId: $parentId, name: $name);
                }

                $target = $data->targetNode;
                $file = null;
                $previousHead = null;

                if ($target instanceof Node) {
                    $this->tree->sync($target, $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$target->id]), $target->id));
                    /** @var File $currentFile */
                    $currentFile = $target->file()->firstOrFail();
                    $file = $this->files->lockForUpdate($currentFile);
                    $previousHead = $file->currentVersion()->first();
                }

                $size = $staged->sizeBytes();
                $delta = $size - ($previousHead?->size_bytes ?? 0);

                if ($delta > 0) {
                    $this->storageStore->reserveBytes(storage: $data->storage, delta: $delta);
                } elseif ($delta < 0) {
                    $this->storageStore->adjustUsedBytes(storage: $data->storage, delta: $delta);
                }

                $storageId = $data->storage->getKey();
                if (!is_int($storageId) && !is_string($storageId)) {
                    throw new LogicException('An upload requires a persisted storage identifier.');
                }

                $blob = $this->blobs->commitStaged(
                    staged: $staged,
                    scope: BlobScope::forUpload(
                        Config::dedupScope(),
                        $data->storage->tenant_id === null ? null : (string) $data->storage->tenant_id,
                        (string) $storageId,
                    ),
                    disk: $data->storage->disk,
                );

                return $file instanceof File
                    ? $this->commitNewVersion(data: $data, file: $file, previousHead: $previousHead, blob: $blob, size: $size)
                    : $this->commitNewFile(data: $data, blob: $blob, size: $size, name: $name ?? '', parentId: $parentId ?? throw new LogicException('An upload requires a destination.'));
            }, 3);

            $this->metrics->counter(
                name: 'vaulter_upload_bytes_total',
                amount: $staged->sizeBytes(),
                labels: ['protocol' => 'single'],
            );

            return $node;
        } finally {
            $staged->discard();
        }
    }

    /**
     * New-file path (§2.4): node(kind=file) → file → version_no=1 → edge → repoint.
     */
    private function commitNewFile(UploadFileData $data, Blob $blob, int $size, string $name, int|string $parentId): Node
    {
        $node = $this->nodeStore->create([
            'storage_id' => $data->storage->id,
            'parent_id' => $parentId,
            'kind' => NodeKind::File,
            'name' => $name,
            'mime_type' => $data->mimeType,
            'extension' => $this->extension($data->originalName),
            'size_bytes' => $size,
            'owner_id' => $data->actorId,
            'created_by' => $data->actorId,
        ]);

        $file = $this->files->create([
            'node_id' => $node->id,
            'mime_type' => $data->mimeType,
            'size_bytes' => $size,
            'version_count' => 0,
        ]);

        $version = $this->makeVersion(data: $data, file: $file, blob: $blob, size: $size, versionNo: 1);

        $this->blobs->attach($version, $blob);

        $this->files->repointHead(
            file: $file,
            currentVersionId: $version->id,
            mimeType: $data->mimeType,
            sizeBytes: $size,
            versionNo: $version->version_no,
        );

        $this->nodeStore->update($node, [
            'file_id' => $file->id,
            'current_version_id' => $version->id,
        ]);

        event(new FileUploaded(node: $node->refresh(), file: $file, version: $version, actorId: $data->actorId));

        return $node;
    }

    /**
     * New-version path (§2.4): bump version_no, new version + edge against the
     * (deduped) blob, mark the prior head superseded, repoint file + node heads.
     */
    private function commitNewVersion(UploadFileData $data, File $file, ?FileVersion $previousHead, Blob $blob, int $size): Node
    {
        $node = $data->targetNode;

        if (!$node instanceof Node) {
            // Defensive: isNewVersion() already guaranteed this; keeps PHPStan honest.
            throw new LogicException('[Vaulter] A new-version upload requires a target node.');
        }

        $version = $this->makeVersion(
            data: $data,
            file: $file,
            blob: $blob,
            size: $size,
            versionNo: $file->version_count + 1,
        );

        $this->blobs->attach($version, $blob);

        if ($previousHead instanceof FileVersion) {
            $this->versions->markSuperseded($previousHead);
        }

        $this->files->repointHead(
            file: $file,
            currentVersionId: $version->id,
            mimeType: $data->mimeType ?? $file->mime_type,
            sizeBytes: $size,
            versionNo: $version->version_no,
        );

        $this->nodeStore->repointHead(
            node: $node,
            currentVersionId: $version->id,
            mimeType: $data->mimeType ?? $node->mime_type,
            extension: $node->extension,
            sizeBytes: $size,
        );

        event(new NodeVersionCommitted(
            node: $node->refresh(),
            file: $file,
            version: $version,
            versionNo: $version->version_no,
            actorId: $data->actorId,
        ));

        return $node;
    }

    private function makeVersion(
        UploadFileData $data,
        File $file,
        Blob $blob,
        int $size,
        int $versionNo,
    ): FileVersion {
        return $this->versions->create([
            'file_id' => $file->getKey(),
            'version_no' => $versionNo,
            'blob_id' => $blob->getKey(),
            'content_sha256' => $blob->sha256,
            'size_bytes' => $size,
            'mime_type' => $data->mimeType,
            'original_name' => $data->originalName,
            'status' => 'committed',
            'created_by' => $data->actorId,
            'committed_at' => now(),
        ]);
    }

    /**
     * Reject the upload BEFORE committing bytes when it would push used_bytes past
     * quota_bytes (§1.5). A null quota is unlimited; a non-positive delta (a
     * smaller new version) never trips the check.
     */
    private function assertWithinQuota(UploadFileData $data, int $delta): void
    {
        $quota = $data->storage->quota_bytes;

        if ($quota === null || $delta <= 0) {
            return;
        }

        if ($data->storage->used_bytes + $delta > $quota) {
            throw QuotaExceededException::forStorage(
                storageId: $data->storage->id,
                quotaBytes: $quota,
                usedBytes: $data->storage->used_bytes,
                incomingBytes: $delta,
            );
        }
    }

    /**
     * The destination folder is locked before the bytes are committed, so it cannot
     * be trashed or replaced by a file between the check and the insert.
     */
    private function assertDestination(UploadFileData $data, int|string $parentId, string $name): void
    {
        $parent = $this->tree->locked($this->tree->lock([$parentId]), $parentId);
        if (!$this->tree->sameStorage($parent, $data->storage->id)) {
            throw CrossStorageMoveException::between(fromStorageId: $data->storage->id, toStorageId: $parent->storage_id);
        }

        $this->tree->assertActiveFolder($parent);

        if ($this->nodeRead->siblingNameExists(storage: $data->storage, parentId: $parentId, name: $name)) {
            throw NodeNameConflictException::forName(name: $name, parentId: $parentId);
        }
    }

    private function extension(string $name): ?string
    {
        $ext = pathinfo($name, PATHINFO_EXTENSION);

        return $ext === '' ? null : mb_strtolower($ext);
    }
}

```

/**

        public Node $node,
        public ?Node $into = null,
        public ?string $name = null,
        public int|string|null $actorId = null,
    ) {}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Data/UploadFileData.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Data;

use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Storage;

/**
 * Command for UploadFileAction (§2.4 commit flow). Carries an already-normalised,
 * seekable byte stream plus the upload metadata — the StreamInterface / SplFileInfo
 * / UploadedFile ergonomics are normalised to a plain resource at the facade
 * (DriveContext::upload) so this command stays free of HTTP/PSR types and the
 * Action receives only bytes + intent.
 *
 * Two target shapes share one command:
 *   - new file  → $into set (or null = storage root) + $name; $targetNode null;
 *   - new version → $targetNode set (the existing kind=file node); $into/$name
 *     are ignored (the node keeps its place and name).
 */
final readonly class UploadFileData
{
    /**
     * @param resource $stream a seekable byte stream the BlobStore can stage
     */
    public function __construct(
        public Storage $storage,
        public mixed $stream,
        public string $originalName,
        public ?string $mimeType = null,
        public ?Node $into = null,
        public ?string $name = null,
        public ?Node $targetNode = null,
        public int|string|null $actorId = null,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Whether this upload commits a new version onto an existing file node rather
     * than creating a brand-new file.
     */
    public function isNewVersion(): bool
    {
        return $this->targetNode !== null;
    }

    /**
     * The stored node name — the explicit override, else the original upload name.
     */
    public function resolvedName(): string
    {
        return $this->name ?? $this->originalName;
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/DriveContext.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Collection;
use RuntimeException;
use SplFileInfo;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Drive\DriveHandle;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\CopyNodeAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\CreateFolderAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\ForceDeleteNodeAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\MoveNodeAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\RenameNodeAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\RestoreNodeAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\TrashNodeAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\UploadFileAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\CopyNodeData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\CreateDriveFolderData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\MoveNodeData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\RenameNodeData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\UploadFileData;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Storage;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeReadRepository;

/**
 * The ergonomic Drive handle bound to one (Storage, panel) for a host entity —
 * the surface `Vaulter::for($entity)` returns. Every method is a thin delegation
 * to a final readonly Action (write) or NodeReadRepository (read); the handle adds
 * no business logic, only the convenience of a pre-resolved storage + the
 * stream-normalisation that keeps the Actions free of HTTP/PSR types.
 *
 * Authorization (M2a / H.2) is enforced at the HTTP controller boundary
 * (NodeController + AccessResolver) before these methods are reached — the handle
 * itself stays a pure delegation surface and performs no access checks, mirroring
 * the legacy DocumentVisibilityService placement canon.
 */
final readonly class DriveContext implements DriveHandle
{
    public function __construct(
        private Storage $storage,
        private string $panel,
        private StorageManager $storages,
        private NodeReadRepository $nodes,
        private CreateFolderAction $createFolder,
        private UploadFileAction $uploadFile,
        private MoveNodeAction $moveNode,
        private RenameNodeAction $renameNode,
        private CopyNodeAction $copyNode,
        private TrashNodeAction $trashNode,
        private RestoreNodeAction $restoreNode,
        private ForceDeleteNodeAction $forceDeleteNode,
    ) {}

    public function storage(): Storage
    {
        return $this->storage;
    }

    public function panel(): string
    {
        return $this->panel;
    }

    /**
     * The storage's root folder node (§2.1).
     */
    public function root(): Node
    {
        return $this->storages->root($this->storage);
    }

    public function createFolder(string $name, ?Node $parent = null, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {
        return $this->createFolder->execute(new CreateDriveFolderData(
            storage: $this->storage,
            name: $name,
            parent: $parent ?? $this->root(),
            actorId: $actorId,
        ));
    }

    /**
     * Upload bytes into the Drive (§2.4). $source may be a PSR StreamInterface, an
     * SplFileInfo / UploadedFile, an open stream resource, or a raw string — all
     * normalised to a seekable resource before the Action stages and dedups it.
     * $into is the destination folder (null = root); $name overrides the stored
     * name. Typed mixed so core need not hard-require psr/http-message.
     */
    public function upload(
        mixed $source,
        ?Node $into = null,
        ?string $name = null,
        ?string $mimeType = null,
        ?string $originalName = null,
        int|string|null $actorId = null,
    ): Node {
        $stream = $this->toStream($source);
        $resolvedOriginalName = $originalName ?? $this->originalNameFor($source) ?? ($name ?? 'upload.bin');

        return $this->uploadFile->execute(new UploadFileData(
            storage: $this->storage,
            stream: $stream,
            originalName: $resolvedOriginalName,
            mimeType: $mimeType ?? $this->mimeTypeFor($source),
            into: $into ?? $this->root(),
            name: $name,
            actorId: $actorId,
        ));
    }

    /**
     * Upload a NEW VERSION onto an existing file node (§2.4): version_no bumps,
     * the head repoints, the prior version is superseded.
     */
    public function uploadVersion(
        Node $target,
        mixed $source,
        ?string $mimeType = null,
        ?string $originalName = null,
        int|string|null $actorId = null,
    ): Node {
        $stream = $this->toStream($source);
        $resolvedOriginalName = $originalName ?? $this->originalNameFor($source) ?? $target->name;

        return $this->uploadFile->execute(new UploadFileData(
            storage: $this->storage,
            stream: $stream,
            originalName: $resolvedOriginalName,
            mimeType: $mimeType ?? $this->mimeTypeFor($source),
            targetNode: $target,
            actorId: $actorId,
        ));
    }

    public function move(Node $node, ?Node $into = null, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {
        return $this->moveNode->execute(new MoveNodeData(
            node: $node,
            into: $into ?? $this->root(),
            actorId: $actorId,
        ));
    }

    public function rename(Node $node, string $name, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {
        return $this->renameNode->execute(new RenameNodeData(
            node: $node,
            name: $name,
            actorId: $actorId,
        ));
    }

    public function copy(Node $node, ?Node $into = null, ?string $name = null, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {
        return $this->copyNode->execute(new CopyNodeData(
            node: $node,
            into: $into ?? $this->root(),
            name: $name,
            actorId: $actorId,
        ));
    }

    public function trash(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {
        return $this->trashNode->execute(node: $node, actorId: $actorId);
    }

    public function restore(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): Node
    {
        return $this->restoreNode->execute(node: $node, actorId: $actorId);
    }

    public function forceDelete(Node $node, int|string|null $actorId = null): void
    {
        $this->forceDeleteNode->execute(node: $node, actorId: $actorId);
    }

    /**
     * Direct children of a folder (or the root when null) — the hot listing.
     *
     * @return Collection<int, Node>
     */
    public function list(?Node $folder = null): Collection
    {
        return $this->nodes->children(storage: $this->storage, parent: $folder ?? $this->root())->get();
    }

    /**
     * Normalise an upload source to a seekable byte stream resource. A PSR
     * StreamInterface is detached / re-read; an SplFileInfo / UploadedFile is
     * opened; an already-open resource is used as-is.
     *
     * @return resource
     */
    private function toStream(mixed $source): mixed
    {
        if (is_resource($source)) {
            return $source;
        }

        // PSR-7 StreamInterface — detected without a hard dependency: core does
        // not require psr/http-message, so we guard on its presence at runtime.
        if (is_object($source) && is_a($source, 'Psr\Http\Message\StreamInterface')) {
            return $this->streamFromString((string) $source);
        }

        if ($source instanceof SplFileInfo) {
            $handle = @fopen($source->getPathname(), 'rb');

            if ($handle === false) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Unable to open upload source [{$source->getPathname()}].");
            }

            return $handle;
        }

        if (is_string($source)) {
            return $this->streamFromString($source);
        }

        throw new RuntimeException(
            'Vaulter upload source must be a stream resource, PSR StreamInterface, SplFileInfo/UploadedFile or string.',
        );
    }

    /**
     * @return resource
     */
    private function streamFromString(string $bytes): mixed
    {
        $stream = fopen('php://temp', 'r+b');

        if ($stream === false) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Unable to open an in-memory stream for the upload.');
        }

        fwrite($stream, $bytes);
        rewind($stream);

        return $stream;
    }

    private function originalNameFor(mixed $source): ?string
    {
        if ($source instanceof SplFileInfo) {
            // UploadedFile exposes the client's original filename; SplFileInfo
            // falls back to the on-disk basename.
            if (method_exists($source, 'getClientOriginalName')) {
                /** @var string $name */
                $name = $source->getClientOriginalName();

                return $name;
            }

            return $source->getFilename();
        }

        return null;
    }

    private function mimeTypeFor(mixed $source): ?string
    {
        if ($source instanceof SplFileInfo && method_exists($source, 'getMimeType')) {
            /** @var string|null $mime */
            $mime = $source->getMimeType();

            return $mime;
        }

        return null;
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Exceptions/QuotaExceededException.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions;

use Vaulter\Exceptions\Contracts\ProblemException;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\VaulterException;

/**
 * The upload would push v_storages.used_bytes past v_storages.quota_bytes (§1.5,
 * eager quota). Checked BEFORE the blob is committed so we never store bytes we
 * must immediately reject. A null quota means unlimited and never throws.
 */
final class QuotaExceededException extends VaulterException implements ProblemException
{
    private const string PROBLEM_CODE = 'quota_exceeded';

    private const int HTTP_STATUS = 413;

    public static function forStorage(int|string $storageId, int $quotaBytes, int $usedBytes, int $incomingBytes): self
    {
        return new self(
            "[Vaulter] Upload of {$incomingBytes} bytes exceeds the quota for storage [{$storageId}] ".
            "(used {$usedBytes} of {$quotaBytes}). ".
            'Fix: raise v_storages.quota_bytes, free space, or set quota_bytes to null for unlimited. '.
            'Docs: '.self::docs('quota-exceeded'),
        );
    }

    public function problemCode(): string
    {
        return self::PROBLEM_CODE;
    }

    public function httpStatus(): int
    {
        return self::HTTP_STATUS;
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/StorageManager.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Database\UniqueConstraintViolationException;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\StorageCreated;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Storage;
use Vaulter\Panels\PanelRegistry;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\NodeStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\StorageReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\StorageStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * Get-or-create a storage container for a host entity within a panel (§2.1), with
 * automatic root-folder provisioning. The polymorphic owner triple
 * (owner_type, owner_id, panel) is the idempotency key — guarded by the partial
 * UNIQUE index defineStorages adds, so concurrent first-touches converge on one
 * row (the INSERT loser catches the unique violation and re-reads).
 *
 * The storage's defaults come from the panel: disk from config storage.default_disk
 * and storage_type from the resolved Panel. A fresh storage gets a kind=folder
 * root node, and root_node_id is repointed at it once it exists (the schema leaves
 * that pair without a hard FK to dodge the chicken-and-egg insert order).
 */
final readonly class StorageManager
{
    public function __construct(
        private StorageReadRepository $read,
        private StorageStoreRepository $store,
        private NodeStoreRepository $nodes,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Resolve (or provision) the storage mounted on $owner within $panel.
     * Idempotent: a second call returns the same row.
     */
    public function forOwner(Model $owner, string $panel = 'default'): Storage
    {
        $existing = $this->read->forOwner(owner: $owner, panel: $panel);

        if ($existing instanceof Storage) {
            return $existing;
        }

        try {
            return $this->provision(owner: $owner, panel: $panel);
        } catch (UniqueConstraintViolationException) {
            // Lost the create race — the (owner_type, owner_id, panel) unique index
            // is the backbone; re-read the winner.
            $winner = $this->read->forOwner(owner: $owner, panel: $panel);

            if ($winner instanceof Storage) {
                return $winner;
            }

            throw new \RuntimeException(
                'Lost the storage provisioning race but the winning row is not visible.',
            );
        }
    }

    /**
     * Create the storage row + its root folder node in one transaction and repoint
     * root_node_id at the node.
     */
    private function provision(Model $owner, string $panel): Storage
    {
        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($owner, $panel): Storage {
            $storage = $this->store->create([
                'panel' => $panel,
                'owner_type' => $owner->getMorphClass(),
                'owner_id' => $owner->getKey(),
                'name' => $this->defaultName($owner),
                'disk' => Config::defaultDisk(),
                'storage_type' => $this->storageType($panel),
                'used_bytes' => 0,
            ]);

            $root = $this->nodes->create([
                'storage_id' => $storage->id,
                'parent_id' => null,
                'kind' => NodeKind::Folder,
                'name' => '',
                'size_bytes' => 0,
            ]);

            $this->store->setRootNode(storage: $storage, rootNodeId: $root->id);

            event(new StorageCreated(storage: $storage->refresh(), root: $root));

            return $storage;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Resolve the root folder node of a storage, provisioning a missing one
     * (defensive — a storage created outside this manager may lack a root).
     */
    public function root(Storage $storage): Node
    {
        $root = $storage->rootNode()->first();

        if ($root instanceof Node) {
            return $root;
        }

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($storage): Node {
            $root = $this->nodes->create([
                'storage_id' => $storage->id,
                'parent_id' => null,
                'kind' => NodeKind::Folder,
                'name' => '',
                'size_bytes' => 0,
            ]);

            $this->store->setRootNode(storage: $storage, rootNodeId: $root->id);

            return $root;
        });
    }

    private function storageType(string $panel): string
    {
        return app(PanelRegistry::class)->get($panel)->storageTypeKey();
    }

    private function defaultName(Model $owner): string
    {
        return class_basename($owner).' storage';
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/src/Uploads/UploadSessionManager.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Uploads;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Container\Container;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\Exception\TooManyRequestsHttpException;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Contracts\Uploads\UploadProtocolHandler;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\Uploads\UploadInitData;
use Vaulter\Drive\Enums\Uploads\UploadProtocol;
use Vaulter\Drive\Enums\Uploads\UploadStatus;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\UploadSessionAborted;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\UploadSessionExpired;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\UploadSessionInitiated;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\UploadConflictException;
use Vaulter\Drive\Models\Uploads\UploadSession;
use Vaulter\Drive\Repositories\Uploads\UploadSessionReadRepository;
use Vaulter\Drive\Repositories\Uploads\UploadSessionStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Drive\Support\Uploads\StagedUpload;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * The resumable-upload lifecycle coordinator (§2.8 / D16 / M3a). It owns:
 *   - initiate(UploadInitData) → a fresh UploadSession (status=initiated, a UNIQUE
 *     upload_id, expires_at = now + the configured TTL), oversize-rejected up front;
 *   - the lifecycle transitions (initiated → uploading → assembled → committed) plus
 *     abort/expire, each guarded by UploadStatus::canTransitionTo;
 *   - resolving the per-protocol handler (TusUploadHandler / S3MultipartHandler) for
 *     a session.
 *
 * It is read/CLI-context safe: it takes no Request and persists through the store
 * repository (never Model::create) so the controller stays thin and the sweeper /
 * queue can drive it. The actual assemble→commit (BlobStore.commit) is
 * CommitUploadAction — the manager only moves the session's state and cleans up the
 * staged temp + protocol state on abort/expire (H.3).
 */
final readonly class UploadSessionManager
{
    public function __construct(
        private Container $container,
        private UploadSessionStoreRepository $sessions,
        private UploadSessionReadRepository $sessionsRead,
        private StagedUpload $staged,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Open a resumable session (§2.8). Validates the declared total against the
     * configured ceiling BEFORE any bytes are accepted (oversize rejected up front,
     * never staged), checks the actor's active-session cap (P1.3 / F-P1.4-09), mints
     * a UNIQUE upload_id, stamps the TTL, and lets the protocol handler initialise
     * its side (S3 CreateMultipartUpload; a no-op for tus).
     */
    public function initiate(UploadInitData $data): UploadSession
    {
        $this->assertWithinMaxBytes($data->totalSizeBytes);
        $this->assertWithinActiveSessionCap($data->actorId);

        $session = $this->sessions->create([
            'tenant_id' => $this->tenantId($data),
            'storage_id' => $data->storage->getKey(),
            'upload_id' => (string) Str::uuid(),
            'node_id' => $data->targetNode?->getKey(),
            'target_parent_id' => $data->targetParent?->getKey(),
            'target_name' => $data->targetName,
            'protocol' => $data->protocol,
            'status' => UploadStatus::Initiated,
            'expected_sha256' => $data->expectedSha256,
            'total_size_bytes' => $data->totalSizeBytes,
            'received_bytes' => 0,
            'chunk_size' => Config::uploadChunkSize(),
            'mime_type' => $data->mimeType,
            'metadata' => $data->metadata === [] ? null : $data->metadata,
            'expires_at' => Carbon::now()->addHours(Config::uploadSessionTtlHours()),
            'created_by' => $data->actorId,
        ]);

        $this->handlerFor($session)->initialise($session);

        $session = $session->refresh();

        // F-P1.4-13: the lifecycle audit gap — create had no activity trace at all.
        event(new UploadSessionInitiated($session, $data->actorId));

        return $session;
    }

    /**
     * The per-protocol handler for a session (§2.8), resolved from the container by
     * the session's protocol enum so a host may swap either implementation.
     */
    public function handlerFor(UploadSession $session): UploadProtocolHandler
    {
        return $this->handlerForProtocol($session->protocol);
    }

    /**
     * The handler for a protocol value (used before a session exists).
     */
    public function handlerForProtocol(UploadProtocol $protocol): UploadProtocolHandler
    {
        return match ($protocol) {
            UploadProtocol::Tus, UploadProtocol::Direct => $this->container->make(TusUploadHandler::class),
            UploadProtocol::S3Multipart => $this->container->make(S3MultipartHandler::class),
        };
    }

    /**
     * Mark a session assembled (§2.8) once its handler reports every byte received —
     * the gate the commit step waits on. A no-op (returns as-is) when the session is
     * not actually complete, so a premature complete() call cannot skip ahead.
     */
    public function markAssembled(UploadSession $session): UploadSession
    {
        if ($session->status === UploadStatus::Assembled) {
            return $session;
        }

        $this->guardTransition($session, UploadStatus::Assembled);

        return $this->sessions->transition($session, UploadStatus::Assembled);
    }

    /**
     * Drive assemble→commit INSIDE the same critical section as a tus PATCH (VLT-REMED
     * P5.2 / R-11). The controller used to do it in the open: `markAssembled()` then
     * `CommitUploadAction::execute()`, each on its own connection-level transaction, with
     * nothing holding the session lock in between — while {@see reclaim()} explicitly
     * reclaims an Assembled session and {@see UploadSession::scopeSweepable()} lists one
     * past its TTL. A sweeper tick therefore took the lock unopposed and discarded the
     * staged object MID-STREAM: the commit either died on "no staged bytes" or wrote a
     * TRUNCATED blob (the CAS re-hashes whatever it is handed — nothing downstream could
     * tell). Holding the lock across the whole "assemble → BlobStore.commit → markCommitted"
     * window makes the sweeper's non-blocking acquire fail instead, which it already reads
     * as "skip, this session is alive".
     *
     * The status is RE-READ under the lock: a session reclaimed between the last chunk and
     * this call is refused with a conflict and $commit is never invoked — no bytes of a
     * terminal session ever reach the Drive.
     *
     * @template TResult
     *
     * @param  callable(UploadSession): TResult $commit
     * @return TResult
     */
    public function commitAssembled(UploadSession $session, callable $commit): mixed
    {
        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($session, $commit): mixed {
            $locked = $this->sessions->acquireForPatch($session);

            if ($locked->status !== UploadStatus::Assembled) {
                if (!$locked->status->canTransitionTo(UploadStatus::Assembled)) {
                    throw UploadConflictException::forClosedSession($locked->upload_id);
                }

                $locked = $this->sessions->transition($locked, UploadStatus::Assembled);
            }

            return $commit($locked);
        });
    }

    /**
     * Abort a session (§2.8): reclaim the protocol-side state (S3 AbortMultipartUpload),
     * discard the staged temp, and tombstone the row as Aborted. Idempotent — a
     * terminal session is returned untouched (and, since nothing changed, emits no
     * activity — F-P1.4-13 traces state CHANGES, not no-op calls).
     *
     * Runs inside the SAME critical section as a tus PATCH (VLT-REMED P5.2 / R-02) —
     * see {@see reclaim()}. Throws UploadConflictException when a PATCH holds the lock.
     */
    public function abort(UploadSession $session, int|string|null $actorId = null): UploadSession
    {
        return $this->reclaim(
            session: $session,
            terminal: UploadStatus::Aborted,
            // F-P1.4-13: the lifecycle audit gap — abort had no activity trace at all.
            audit: static function (UploadSession $aborted) use ($actorId): void {
                event(new UploadSessionAborted($aborted, $actorId));
            },
        );
    }

    /**
     * Expire a stale session (§2.8 sweeper): the same cleanup as abort but the
     * terminal state is Expired (so the audit distinguishes a swept session from a
     * client-cancelled one). Runs outside an HTTP request (console/schedule) — the
     * activity row carries no actor (a system reclaim).
     *
     * Runs inside the SAME critical section as a tus PATCH (VLT-REMED P5.2 / R-02) —
     * see {@see reclaim()}. Throws UploadConflictException when a PATCH holds the lock;
     * the sweeper treats that as "skip, it is alive", not as a failure.
     */
    public function expire(UploadSession $session): UploadSession
    {
        return $this->reclaim(
            session: $session,
            terminal: UploadStatus::Expired,
            // F-P1.4-13: the lifecycle audit gap — expire had no activity trace at all.
            audit: static function (UploadSession $expired): void {
                event(new UploadSessionExpired($expired));
            },
        );
    }

    /**
     * The shared abort/expire body — and the reason both exist at all: reclaiming a
     * session DESTROYS bytes (staged temp discard) and moves the row to a terminal
     * state, which is exactly the window a concurrent tus PATCH is writing into
     * (VLT-REMED P5.2 / R-02).
     *
     * Before the fix, expire() ran with no lock at all: the sweeper could delete the
     * staged object between a PATCH's "read offset" and its "append", so the PATCH wrote
     * its chunk alone (losing the accepted prefix) and then forced the row back to
     * Uploading — a resurrected session pointing at a truncated file, with no error
     * anywhere. The barrier therefore has to stand on the path that DELIVERS the harm:
     *
     *   1. one transaction, taking the SAME per-session lock as the PATCH as its first
     *      statement (a PATCH in flight → UploadConflictException, no wait);
     *   2. the status is RE-READ under that lock (the row may have moved since the
     *      caller/sweeper selected it) — a session that is no longer reclaimable is
     *      returned untouched, and nothing is discarded;
     *   3. only then are the protocol state and the staged bytes released.
     *
     * @param callable(UploadSession): void $audit
     */
    private function reclaim(UploadSession $session, UploadStatus $terminal, callable $audit): UploadSession
    {
        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($session, $terminal, $audit): UploadSession {
            $locked = $this->sessions->acquireForPatch($session);

            // Re-read under the lock: another actor may have reclaimed/committed it
            // between the caller's SELECT and our lock. Terminal → no bytes to discard.
            if (!$locked->status->isOpen() && $locked->status !== UploadStatus::Assembled) {
                return $locked;
            }

            $this->handlerFor($locked)->abort($locked);
            $this->staged->discard($locked);

            $reclaimed = $this->sessions->transition($locked, $terminal);

            $audit($reclaimed);

            return $reclaimed;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Reject an oversize upload at initiate (§2.8 / HARDENING): a declared total past
     * the configured ceiling never opens a session, so we never stage bytes we must
     * reject. A null total (size unknown up front) is allowed; the tus PATCH path
     * still re-checks the ceiling as the offset grows.
     */
    private function assertWithinMaxBytes(?int $totalSizeBytes): void
    {
        $max = Config::uploadMaxBytes();

        if ($max === 0 || $totalSizeBytes === null) {
            return;
        }

        if ($totalSizeBytes > $max) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'total_size_bytes' => "The upload size {$totalSizeBytes} exceeds the maximum allowed {$max} bytes.",
            ]);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Refuse a new session past the configured cap on OPEN sessions per actor
     * (§2.8 / P1.3 / F-P1.4-09) — an actor may not open an unbounded number of
     * concurrent staging areas. 0 disables the cap; a null actor id (unattributed/
     * system-minted session) is never capped (there is no per-actor bucket).
     */
    private function assertWithinActiveSessionCap(int|string|null $actorId): void
    {
        $max = Config::maxActiveUploadSessions();

        if ($max === 0 || $actorId === null) {
            return;
        }

        if ($this->sessionsRead->countActiveForActor($actorId) >= $max) {
            throw new TooManyRequestsHttpException(
                retryAfter: null,
                message: "Too many active upload sessions ({$max} max) for this actor.",
            );
        }
    }

    private function guardTransition(UploadSession $session, UploadStatus $target): void
    {
        if (!$session->status->canTransitionTo($target)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'status' => "Cannot transition upload session from {$session->status->value} to {$target->value}.",
            ]);
        }
    }

    private function tenantId(UploadInitData $data): int|string|null
    {
        return $data->storage->tenant_id;
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/Concurrency/LockOrderRaceTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Drive\Actions\MoveNodeAction;
use Vaulter\Drive\Data\MoveNodeData;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency\ForkRace;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

it('V21 completes ten overlapping move/upload races with an acyclic tree and exact head bytes', function (): void {
    ForkRace::skipUnlessSupported($this);
    Storage::fake('local');
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $folder = $drive->createFolder('F');
    for ($i = 0; $i < 10; $i++) {
        $moving = $drive->createFolder('A'.$i);
        $outcomes = ForkRace::run([
            static fn (): string => (string) app(MoveNodeAction::class)->execute(new MoveNodeData(node: $moving, into: $folder))->id,
            static fn (): string => (string) $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('upload '.$i), into: $folder, originalName: $i.'.txt')->id,
        ]);
        foreach ($outcomes as $outcome) {
            expect($outcome->ok)->toBeTrue($outcome->message ?? '');
        }
        foreach (Node::query()->get() as $node) {
            $seen = [];
            while ($node->parent_id !== null) {
                expect(in_array($node->id, $seen, true))->toBeFalse('cycle at node '.$node->id);
                $seen[] = $node->id;
                $node = Node::query()->findOrFail($node->parent_id);
            }
            expect($node->id)->toBe($drive->root()->id);
        }
        expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe((int) File::query()->sum('size_bytes'));
    }
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/Concurrency/QuotaRaceTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\QuotaExceededException;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersionBlob;
use Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency\ForkRace;
use Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency\RaceOutcome;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

it('V02 admits exactly one 8 MiB upload into 10 MiB of free quota', function (): void {
    ForkRace::skipUnlessSupported($this);
    Storage::fake('local');
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $drive->storage()->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 10 * 1024 * 1024])->save();
    // Different parents prevent the destination lock from hiding a broken quota UPDATE.
    $parents = [$drive->createFolder('A'), $drive->createFolder('B')];
    $outcomes = ForkRace::run(array_map(
        static fn (int $i): Closure => static fn (): string => (string) $drive->upload(
            DriveTestHelpers::streamFor(str_repeat((string) $i, 8 * 1024 * 1024)),
            into: $parents[$i], originalName: $i.'.bin',
        )->getKey(),
        [0, 1],
    ));
    $won = array_values(array_filter($outcomes, static fn (RaceOutcome $o): bool => $o->ok));
    $lost = array_values(array_filter($outcomes, static fn (RaceOutcome $o): bool => !$o->ok));
    expect($won)->toHaveCount(1)->and($lost)->toHaveCount(1);
    expect($lost[0]->failedWith(QuotaExceededException::class))->toBeTrue($lost[0]->message ?? '');
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(8 * 1024 * 1024)
        ->and((int) File::query()->sum('size_bytes'))->toBe(8 * 1024 * 1024)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(FileVersionBlob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
    $blob = Blob::query()->sole();
    // Age the actual disk object, not Carbon: orphan grace uses filesystem mtime.
    touch(Storage::disk('local')->path($blob->storage_key), time() - 48 * 3600);
    $this->artisan('vaulter:reconcile-blobs', ['--orphans' => true, '--prune' => true, '--orphan-grace' => 24])->assertSuccessful();
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
    expect(hash_file('sha256', Storage::disk('local')->path($blob->storage_key)))->toBe($blob->sha256);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/Concurrency/RevisionNumberRaceTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency\ForkRace;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    ForkRace::skipUnlessSupported($this);
    Storage::fake('local');
});

it('V03 serializes revisions and accounts for their actual preceding heads', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('old'), originalName: 'a.txt');
    // Both children inherit revision 1, even after the winner commits revision 2.
    $outcomes = ForkRace::run(array_map(
        static fn (string $bytes): Closure => static fn (): string => (string) $drive->uploadVersion(
            $node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), originalName: 'a.txt',
        )->current_version_id,
        ['short', 'a much longer head'],
    ));
    foreach ($outcomes as $outcome) {
        expect($outcome->ok)->toBeTrue($outcome->message ?? '');
    }
    $file = $node->file()->firstOrFail();
    $head = $file->currentVersion;
    expect($file->versions()->orderBy('version_no')->pluck('version_no')->all())->toBe([1, 2, 3])
        ->and($file->version_count)->toBe(3)
        ->and($head->version_no)->toBe(3)
        ->and($node->refresh()->current_version_id)->toBe($head->id)
        ->and($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe($head->size_bytes)
        ->and($node->size_bytes)->toBe($head->size_bytes);
});

// Upload's node lock ALSO serializes revisions. Exercise the aggregate repository
// independently so removing its FOR UPDATE is a meaningful negative control.
it('V03 aggregate lock protects counter allocation even without a caller node lock', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('head'), originalName: 'a.txt');
    $file = $node->file;
    $outcomes = ForkRace::run(array_map(
        static fn (int $i): Closure => static fn (): int => app(VaulterDatabase::class)->transaction(function () use ($file): int {
            $files = app(FileStoreRepository::class);
            $locked = $files->lockForUpdate($file);
            $head = $locked->currentVersion;
            // Widen the read/write overlap: an unlocked reader also sees counter 1.
            usleep(100_000);
            $versionNo = $locked->version_count + 1;
            $attributes = $head->getAttributes();
            unset($attributes[$head->getKeyName()]);
            $attributes['version_no'] = $versionNo;
            $version = app(FileVersionStoreRepository::class)->create($attributes);
            $files->repointHead($locked, $version->id, $head->mime_type, $head->size_bytes, $versionNo);

            return $versionNo;
        }),
        [0, 1],
    ));
    foreach ($outcomes as $outcome) {
        expect($outcome->ok)->toBeTrue($outcome->message ?? '');
    }
    $numbers = array_map(static fn ($o): int => $o->value, $outcomes);
    sort($numbers);
    expect($numbers)->toBe([2, 3])
        ->and(File::query()->findOrFail($file->id)->version_count)->toBe(3)
        ->and(FileVersion::query()->where('file_id', $file->id)->orderBy('version_no')->pluck('version_no')->all())->toBe([1, 2, 3]);
});

it('reuses prepared bytes on a serialization retry and discards the temp file', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $before = glob(sys_get_temp_dir().'/vaulter_cas_*') ?: [];
    $injected = false;
    app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection()->listen(function ($query) use (&$injected): void {
        if (!$injected && str_starts_with($query->sql, 'insert into "v_files"')) {
            $injected = true;
            throw new PDOException('serialization failure', 40001);
        }
    });
    $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('retry the same bytes'), originalName: 'a.txt');
    expect($injected)->toBeTrue()
        ->and($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(20)
        ->and(File::query()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(FileVersion::query()->sole()->version_no)->toBe(1)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(glob(sys_get_temp_dir().'/vaulter_cas_*') ?: [])->toBe($before);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/Concurrency/SameNameUploadRaceTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency\ForkRace;
use Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency\RaceOutcome;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

it('V20 creates one x.pdf and returns a domain conflict to the second writer', function (): void {
    ForkRace::skipUnlessSupported($this);
    Storage::fake('local');
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $outcomes = ForkRace::run([
        static fn (): string => (string) $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('first'), originalName: 'x.pdf')->id,
        static fn (): string => (string) $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('second'), originalName: 'x.pdf')->id,
    ]);
    $won = array_values(array_filter($outcomes, static fn (RaceOutcome $o): bool => $o->ok));
    $lost = array_values(array_filter($outcomes, static fn (RaceOutcome $o): bool => !$o->ok));
    expect($won)->toHaveCount(1)->and($lost)->toHaveCount(1);
    expect($lost[0]->failedWith(NodeNameConflictException::class))->toBeTrue($lost[0]->message ?? '');
    expect(Node::query()->where('name', 'x.pdf')->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/Drive/CopyNodeTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Exceptions\Nodes\NodeNameConflictException;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('копия файла переиспользует ОДИН блоб через новое ребро', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $original = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('shared content'), originalName: 'a.txt');

    $copy = $drive->copy($original, name: 'a-copy.txt');

    expect($copy->kind)->toBe(NodeKind::File)
        ->and($copy->name)->toBe('a-copy.txt')
        ->and($copy->getKey())->not->toEqual($original->getKey())
        // One physical blob, two File handles, two versions referencing it.
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(File::query()->count())->toBe(2);

    $blob = Blob::query()->first();
    expect($blob->versions()->count())->toBe(2);
});

it('глубокая копия папки клонирует всё поддерево', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $root = $drive->createFolder('Project');
    $sub = $drive->createFolder('Sub', $root);
    $drive->createFolder('Leaf', $sub);
    $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('file bytes'), into: $sub, originalName: 'data.bin');

    $copy = $drive->copy($root, name: 'Project Copy');

    // 4 source nodes + 4 copied = 8 (Project, Sub, Leaf, data.bin) ×2.
    expect(Node::query()->where('kind', '!=', NodeKind::Folder->value)->count())->toBe(2) // two file nodes
        ->and(Node::query()->count())->toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(9); // + storage root

    $copiedSub = $copy->children()->where('name', 'Sub')->first();
    expect($copiedSub)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($copiedSub->children()->count())->toBe(2); // Leaf + data.bin

    // Folder deep-copy of a file still shares the single blob.
    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('copy отклоняет конфликт имён в месте назначения', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $a = $drive->createFolder('A');
    $drive->createFolder('B');

    expect(fn () => $drive->copy($a, name: 'B'))
        ->toThrow(NodeNameConflictException::class);
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/Drive/UploadFileTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Event;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\FileUploaded;
use Vaulter\Drive\Events\NodeVersionCommitted;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\QuotaExceededException;
use Vaulter\Enums\Drive\NodeKind;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('загружает файл: node kind=file + File + FileVersion + ребро', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('hello drive'), originalName: 'hello.txt', mimeType: 'text/plain');

    expect($node->kind)->toBe(NodeKind::File)
        ->and($node->name)->toBe('hello.txt')
        ->and($node->extension)->toBe('txt')
        ->and($node->mime_type)->toBe('text/plain')
        ->and($node->size_bytes)->toBe(11)
        ->and($node->file_id)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and($node->current_version_id)->not->toBeNull()
        ->and(FileVersion::query()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
});

it('дедуп: два одинаковых файла → один блоб, два узла, два ребра', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $a = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('same bytes'), originalName: 'a.bin');
    $b = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('same bytes'), originalName: 'b.bin');

    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1)
        ->and(Node::query()->where('kind', NodeKind::File->value)->count())->toBe(2);

    $blob = Blob::query()->first();

    expect($blob->versions()->count())->toBe(2)
        ->and($a->getKey())->not->toEqual($b->getKey());
});

it('увеличивает used_bytes в рамках транзакции загрузки', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('1234567890'), originalName: 'x.bin');

    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(10);
});

it('квота: загрузка сверх квоты отклоняется ДО коммита блоба', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $drive->storage()->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 5])->save();

    expect(fn () => $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('too many bytes'), originalName: 'x.bin'))
        ->toThrow(QuotaExceededException::class);

    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(0)
        ->and(Node::query()->where('kind', NodeKind::File->value)->count())->toBe(0)
        ->and($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(0);
});

it('новая версия: бамп version_no, перенаведение head, прежняя superseded', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('v1'), originalName: 'doc.txt');
    $firstVersionId = $node->current_version_id;

    $updated = $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('version two longer'), originalName: 'doc.txt');

    $file = $updated->file;

    expect($file->version_count)->toBe(2)
        ->and($updated->current_version_id)->not->toEqual($firstVersionId)
        ->and(FileVersion::query()->find($firstVersionId)->status)->toBe('superseded')
        ->and($file->currentVersion->version_no)->toBe(2);
});

it('новая версия корректирует used_bytes на дельту размера', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('aaaa'), originalName: 'doc.txt'); // 4 bytes
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(4);

    $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('aaaaaaaaaa'), originalName: 'doc.txt'); // 10 bytes

    // delta = 10 − 4 = 6 → total 10 (the live head size, not the sum of versions).
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(10);
});

it('диспатчит FileUploaded для нового файла и NodeVersionCommitted для версии', function (): void {
    Event::fake([FileUploaded::class, NodeVersionCommitted::class]);

    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('v1'), originalName: 'doc.txt');

    Event::assertDispatched(FileUploaded::class);

    $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('v2 longer'), originalName: 'doc.txt');

    Event::assertDispatched(NodeVersionCommitted::class);
});
