# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/Feature/QuotaReserveTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Exceptions\QuotaExceededException;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\File;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersionBlob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\Node;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\StorageStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Tests\Drive\DriveTestHelpers;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
});

it('reserves from the database value with unlimited and exact-boundary quotas', function (?int $quota): void {
    $storage = DriveTestHelpers::drive()->storage();
    $storage->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => $quota, 'used_bytes' => 2])->save();
    $stale = $storage->fresh();
    $storage->forceFill(['used_bytes' => 4])->save();

    app(VaulterDatabase::class)->transaction(fn () => app(StorageStoreRepository::class)->reserveBytes($stale, 6));

    expect($storage->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(10);
})->with([null, 10]);

it('rejects one byte over the quota without changing used_bytes', function (): void {
    $storage = DriveTestHelpers::drive()->storage();
    $storage->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 10, 'used_bytes' => 10])->save();

    expect(fn () => app(VaulterDatabase::class)->transaction(fn () => app(StorageStoreRepository::class)->reserveBytes($storage, 1)))
        ->toThrow(QuotaExceededException::class);
    expect($storage->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(10);
});

it('releases bytes down to zero and accepts a zero reservation', function (): void {
    $storage = DriveTestHelpers::drive()->storage();
    $storage->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 2, 'used_bytes' => 2])->save();
    $repo = app(StorageStoreRepository::class);

    app(VaulterDatabase::class)->transaction(function () use ($repo, $storage): void {
        $repo->reserveBytes($storage, 0);
        $repo->reserveBytes($storage, -5);
    });
    expect($storage->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(0);
});

it('rolls back an entire subtree copy when its summed heads exceed quota', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $folder = $drive->createFolder('source');
    $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('aaaa'), into: $folder, originalName: 'a.txt');
    $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('bbbbbb'), into: $folder, originalName: 'b.txt');
    $drive->storage()->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 19])->save();
    $counts = [Node::query()->count(), File::query()->count(), FileVersion::query()->count(), FileVersionBlob::query()->count()];

    expect(fn () => $drive->copy($folder, name: 'copy'))->toThrow(QuotaExceededException::class);

    expect([Node::query()->count(), File::query()->count(), FileVersion::query()->count(), FileVersionBlob::query()->count()])->toBe($counts)
        ->and(Node::query()->where('name', 'copy')->exists())->toBeFalse()
        ->and($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(10)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(2);
});

it('uses the locked file head despite stale node size and stale quota snapshots', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('aaaa'), originalName: 'a.txt');
    $stale = $node->fresh();
    $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('abcdefghij'), originalName: 'a.txt');
    $drive->storage()->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 10])->save();
    // The stale node says 4 bytes; the locked file head says 10. Replacing it
    // with 8 bytes releases 2, rather than reserving 4 and rejecting the upload.
    $drive->uploadVersion($stale, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('abcdefgh'), originalName: 'a.txt');
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(8)
        ->and($stale->fresh()->file->version_count)->toBe(3);
});

it('copies a stale file from its current head and reserves that exact size', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('aaaa'), originalName: 'a.txt');
    $stale = $node->fresh();
    $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('abcdefghij'), originalName: 'a.txt');
    $copy = $drive->copy($stale, name: 'copy.txt');
    expect($copy->size_bytes)->toBe(10)
        ->and($copy->file->currentVersion->size_bytes)->toBe(10)
        ->and($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(20);
});

```

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Http\UploadedFile;

/**
 * Streaming-only StorageDriver stub (no presigned URLs) — scoped to this file
 * to avoid colliding with R07StreamingOnlyStorageDriver or StreamingOnlyStorageDriver
 * in sibling test files.
 */
final class R07bStreamingDriver implements StorageDriver
{
    public function __construct(private readonly StorageDriver $inner) {}

    public function disk(?string $disk = null): Filesystem
    {
        return $this->inner->disk($disk);
    }

    public function put(string $key, mixed $stream, ?string $disk = null, bool $ifNoneMatch = false, array $options = []): bool
    {
        return $this->inner->put($key, $stream, $disk, $ifNoneMatch, $options);
    }

    public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->inner->get($key, $disk);
    }

    public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
    {
        return $this->inner->exists($key, $disk);
    }

    public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->inner->delete($key, $disk);
    }

    public function temporaryUrl(string $key, DateTimeInterface $expiresAt, ?string $disk = null): ?string
    {
        return null; // force PHP streaming path
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// L78 MinusToPlus: quota delta uses subtraction (new - old), not addition.
// A version that SHRINKS the file has delta ≤ 0 and must bypass the quota
// check even when used_bytes == quota_bytes after the prior head.
it('UploadFile L78: shrinking version passes when quota is at capacity (delta is new-old, not new+old)', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    // Upload an 8-byte file to fill storage to exactly 8 bytes.
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('12345678'), originalName: 'f.bin');
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(8);

    // Clamp quota to current usage — any positive delta would now fail.
    $drive->storage()->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 8])->save();

    // Upload a 6-byte version (delta = 6 − 8 = −2 ≤ 0) — must NOT throw.
    // MinusToPlus mutant: delta = 6 + 8 = 14 → quota check fires → exception.
    $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('abcdef'), originalName: 'f.bin');

    // used_bytes drops to 6 (delta = −2).
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(6);
});

// L80 IfNegated / L81 RemoveMethodCall: assertNameAvailable is called for new
// files (not new versions), so a sibling-name conflict must be detected.
it('UploadFile L80/L81: sibling-name conflict is rejected for a new-file upload', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('a'), originalName: 'dup.bin');

    // Second upload with the same resolved name must throw.
    // IfNegated mutant: skips assertNameAvailable for non-version uploads → no throw.
    expect(fn () => $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('b'), originalName: 'dup.bin'))
        ->toThrow(NodeNameConflictException::class);
});

// L106 DecrementInteger: delta=0→-1 comparison — a delta of exactly −1 must
// still call adjustUsedBytes; if the condition becomes !== -1, delta=−1 skips
// the adjustment and used_bytes is stuck at the old value.
it('UploadFile L106: used_bytes is decremented when the new version is exactly 1 byte smaller', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('12345'), originalName: 'f.bin'); // 5 bytes
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(5);

    $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('1234'), originalName: 'f.bin'); // 4 bytes, delta = −1
    // DecrementInteger mutant: condition becomes $delta !== -1, which is FALSE for delta=−1
    // → adjustUsedBytes is skipped → used_bytes stays at 5. Test fails on the expect below.
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(4);
});

// L196/L203 CoalesceRemoveLeft: when a new-version upload carries a different
// mimeType, both repointHead calls must use $data->mimeType, not the file's
// stale value ($file->mime_type / $node->mime_type).
it('UploadFile L196/L203: new version with a changed mime type updates node and file mime', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('v1 bytes'), originalName: 'f.bin', mimeType: 'text/plain');

    // Upload a version with a different declared mime.
    $updated = $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('v2 longer bytes'), originalName: 'f.bin', mimeType: 'application/pdf');

    // CoalesceRemoveLeft mutant: $file->mime_type ('text/plain') is used instead.
    expect($updated->refresh()->mime_type)->toBe('application/pdf');
    expect($updated->file()->first()->mime_type)->toBe('application/pdf');
});

// L234-236 RemoveArrayItem: the version row created by makeVersion must carry
// status='committed', the actor id in created_by, and a non-null committed_at.
it('UploadFile L234-236: committed version has status=committed, correct created_by, and committed_at set', function (): void {
    $this->travelTo(now()->startOfSecond());
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();

    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('data'), originalName: 'doc.txt', actorId: 42);

    $version = $node->currentVersion()->first();

        ->and($version->created_by)->toEqual(42)       // L235
        ->and($version->committed_at)->not->toBeNull() // L236

// L249 SmallerOrEqualToSmaller / Decrement: delta=0 must be treated as ≤0 and
// skip the quota check — even when storage is already over quota. If the
// boundary shifts to <0, a delta-0 version upload against an over-quota storage
// incorrectly fires the exception.
it('UploadFile L249 SmallerOrEqualToSmaller/Decrement: same-size version passes even when storage is over quota', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('hello'), originalName: 'f.bin'); // 5 bytes; used=5

    // Force storage into over-quota state (used > quota).
    $drive->storage()->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 4, 'used_bytes' => 5])->save();

    // New version of same 5-byte size → delta = 5 − 5 = 0. Must NOT throw.
    // SmallerOrEqualToSmaller mutant: $delta <= 0 → $delta < 0; delta=0 is not <0
    //   → quota check fires → 5+0 > 4 = true → QuotaExceededException. Test fails.
    $drive->uploadVersion($node, DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('world'), originalName: 'f.bin');

    // used_bytes stays at 5 (delta=0, adjustUsedBytes not called for 0 delta).
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(5);
});

// L249 Increment: delta=1 must NOT be skipped (1 > 0 is true).  With the
// Increment mutant ($delta <= 1), a 1-byte positive delta is treated as ≤0 and
// the check is bypassed — quota is violated silently.
it('UploadFile L249 Increment: a 1-byte over-quota upload is rejected', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $drive->storage()->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 5, 'used_bytes' => 5])->save();

    // delta = 1 − 0 = 1 (new file). Must exceed quota: 5+1 > 5 → throw.
    // Increment mutant: $delta <= 1 → skips check → upload silently succeeds.
    expect(fn () => $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('x'), originalName: 'f.bin'))
        ->toThrow(QuotaExceededException::class);
});

// L312 Increment on probeSize: fstat size+1 causes a false 1-byte quota excess.
// Create a seekable stream of exactly S bytes, used_bytes+S = quota, so the
// upload must pass (used+S ≤ quota). With Increment, probedSize=S+1, delta=S+1,
// used + (S+1) > quota → spurious QuotaExceededException.
it('UploadFile L312 Increment: probeSize probe does not add a phantom byte at the quota boundary', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $drive->storage()->forceFill(['quota_bytes' => 100, 'used_bytes' => 91])->save();

    // Stream of 9 bytes. delta = 9 − 0 = 9. Check: 91+9 = 100, not > 100 → passes.
    // Increment mutant: probedSize=10 → delta=10 → 91+10=101 > 100 → throws. Test fails.
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('123456789'), originalName: 'f.bin');

    expect($node)->toBeInstanceOf(Node::class);
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(100);
});

// L383/L385 RemoveIntegerCast / Decrement / Increment:
// The shared endpoint uses LengthAwarePaginator and exposes meta.per_page, letting
// us assert the EXACT clamped value rather than a result-count proxy.
// Route: GET api/vaulter/shared (fixed segment, gated on features.grants = true by default).
it('NodeController L383/L385: per_page is clamped to exactly 1 at the lower bound and 200 at the upper bound', function (): void {
    // Ensure the grants feature is enabled so the shared route is registered.
    config()->set('vaulter.features.grants', true);

    // per_page=0 → max(1, ...) → 1 exactly.
    // Decrement on 1: max(0, ...) → 0 stored; Increment: max(2, ...) → 2.
    $this->getJson('api/vaulter/shared?per_page=0')
        ->assertOk()

    $this->getJson('api/vaulter/shared?per_page=1')
        ->assertOk()
        ->assertJsonPath('meta.per_page', 1);

    // per_page=200 → min(..., 200) → 200 exactly.
    // Decrement on 200: min(..., 199) → 199 — caught here.
    $this->getJson('api/vaulter/shared?per_page=200')
        ->assertOk()
        ->assertJsonPath('meta.per_page', 200);

    // Requesting over the cap: must be clamped to 200, not 201.
    $this->getJson('api/vaulter/shared?per_page=201')
        ->assertOk()
        ->assertJsonPath('meta.per_page', 200);

// L114 TernaryNegated / NotIdenticalToIdentical: original_name is used as the
// download filename when it is non-empty; the node-name must NOT be used instead.
it('DownloadController L114: non-empty original_name is used as filename, not node name', function (): void {
    $storage = r07bActStorage();

    // Upload so the original file name ('original.txt') differs from the stored name ('stored.txt').
    $nodeId = $this->postJson('api/vaulter/storages/'.$storage->getKey().'/uploads', [
        'file' => UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('original.txt', 'payload'),
        'name' => 'stored.txt',

// L114 EmptyStringToNotEmpty: when original_name is '', the fallback must be node->name.
it('DownloadController L114: empty original_name falls back to node name in the filename', function (): void {
    $storage = r07bActStorage();

    $nodeId = $this->postJson('api/vaulter/storages/'.$storage->getKey().'/uploads', [
        'file' => UploadedFile::fake()->createWithContent('report.txt', 'data'),
    ])->assertCreated()->json('data.id');

    // Force original_name to '' on the current version so the fallback branch is exercised.
    $version = Node::find($nodeId)->currentVersion()->first();
    $version->forceFill(['original_name' => ''])->save();

    app()->instance(StorageDriver::class, new R07bStreamingDriver(app(StorageDriver::class)));

    $response = $this->get('api/vaulter/nodes/'.$nodeId.'/download');
    $response->assertOk();

    // EmptyStringToNotEmpty mutant: '' === ' ' is false → '' returned as filename.
    // Original: '' === '' → true → uses node name 'report.txt'.
    expect($response->headers->get('content-disposition'))->toContain('report.txt');
});

// L115 first CoalesceRemoveLeft: version.mime_type takes precedence over node.mime_type.
// When version.mime_type is non-null, it must be used for Content-Type, not node.mime_type.
it('DownloadController L115 first CoalesceRemoveLeft: version mime_type wins over node mime_type', function (): void {
    $storage = r07bActStorage();

    // Upload with image/png (whitelisted, served as-is).
    $nodeId = $this->postJson('api/vaulter/storages/'.$storage->getKey().'/uploads', [
        'file' => UploadedFile::fake()->create('photo.png', 1, 'image/png'),
    ])->assertCreated()->json('data.id');

    // Force node.mime_type to something different so version.mime_type wins.
    Node::find($nodeId)->forceFill(['mime_type' => 'application/octet-stream'])->save();

    app()->instance(StorageDriver::class, new R07bStreamingDriver(app(StorageDriver::class)));

    $response = $this->get('api/vaulter/nodes/'.$nodeId.'/download');
    $response->assertOk();

    // First CoalesceRemoveLeft mutant: node.mime_type used → 'application/octet-stream'.
    expect($response->headers->get('content-type'))->toBe('image/png');
});

// L115 second CoalesceRemoveLeft: node.mime_type is the fallback when version.mime_type is null.
it('DownloadController L115 second CoalesceRemoveLeft: node mime_type is used when version mime_type is null', function (): void {
    $storage = r07bActStorage();

    $nodeId = $this->postJson('api/vaulter/storages/'.$storage->getKey().'/uploads', [
        'file' => UploadedFile::fake()->create('photo.png', 1, 'image/png'),
    ])->assertCreated()->json('data.id');

    // Set version.mime_type to null so node.mime_type must be the fallback.
    $version = Node::find($nodeId)->currentVersion()->first();
    $version->forceFill(['mime_type' => null])->save();
    // Ensure node.mime_type is set to the whitelisted value we want to observe.
    Node::find($nodeId)->forceFill(['mime_type' => 'image/png'])->save();

    app()->instance(StorageDriver::class, new R07bStreamingDriver(app(StorageDriver::class)));

    $response = $this->get('api/vaulter/nodes/'.$nodeId.'/download');
    $response->assertOk();

    // Second CoalesceRemoveLeft mutant: removes node.mime_type → 'application/octet-stream' fallback.
    expect($response->headers->get('content-type'))->toBe('image/png');
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ForceDeleteNodeAction
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// L61 GreaterToGreaterOrEqual / Increment: freedBytes > 0 check. A 1-byte file
// must still reduce used_bytes. Increment mutant (> 1) skips for freedBytes=1.
it('ForceDelete L61: force-deleting a 1-byte file fully reclaims the single byte from used_bytes', function (): void {
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('x'), originalName: 'tiny.bin'); // 1 byte
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(1);

    $drive->trash($node);

    $drive->forceDelete($node);

    // Increment mutant: freedBytes > 1 → skip adjustUsedBytes → used_bytes stays at 1.
    expect($drive->storage()->refresh()->used_bytes)->toBe(0);
});

// L67 UnwrapArrayValues: nodeIds in NodeForceDeleted must be a list (array_values
// guarantees numeric keys from 0). Without array_values the model-key array from
// modelKeys() is already numerically indexed so this mutation is equivalent —
// noted here for traceability; the assertion documents the list contract.
it('ForceDelete L67: NodeForceDeleted event carries numerically indexed nodeIds list', function (): void {
    Event::fake([NodeForceDeleted::class]);
    $drive = DriveTestHelpers::drive();
    $node = $drive->upload(DriveTestHelpers::streamFor('data'), originalName: 'f.bin');
    $nodeId = $node->getKey();

    $drive->trash($node);

    $drive->forceDelete($node);

    Event::assertDispatched(NodeForceDeleted::class, function (NodeForceDeleted $e) use ($nodeId): bool {
        // Must be a list: keys are 0, 1, 2, … (array_values guarantee).
        $isListIndexed = array_values($e->nodeIds) === $e->nodeIds;

        return $isListIndexed && in_array($nodeId, $e->nodeIds, strict: false);
    });
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/Pest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\DatabaseMigrations;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Tests\PanelTestCase;
use Vaulter\Drive\Tests\TestCase;

uses(TestCase::class, RefreshDatabase::class)->in('Drive', 'Uploads', 'Feature', 'Observability');

// Панели живут отдельной директорией, потому что им нужен ДРУГОЙ TestCase: панель обязана
// попасть в конфиг ДО boot'а (PanelRegistry резолвится в packageBooted с P4.4), а Pest
// разрешает ровно один базовый TestCase на директорию.
uses(PanelTestCase::class, RefreshDatabase::class)->in('Panels');

// The concurrency slice runs on COMMITTED rows (no wrapping transaction) so a forked
// child on its own connection can see the session — RefreshDatabase's per-test
// transaction would hide it. DatabaseMigrations migrates fresh and commits.
uses(TestCase::class, DatabaseMigrations::class)->in('Concurrency');

# COMPLETE FILE packages/drive/tests/TestCase.php

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Drive\Tests;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Orchestra\Testbench\TestCase as OrchestraTestCase;
use Vaulter\Drive\VaulterDriveServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\Media\VaulterMediaServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\VaulterServiceProvider;

abstract class TestCase extends OrchestraTestCase
{
    /**
     * @param  Application              $app
     * @return array<int, class-string>
     */
    protected function getPackageProviders($app): array
    {
        return [
            // Core owns the schema (v_nodes/v_storages/v_files/... + v_upload_*),
            // the Node/Storage models, Access/Sharing/Activity and Support\Config.
            VaulterServiceProvider::class,
            // Media binds the concrete CAS engine behind the Contracts\Cas seam —
            // Drive upload/download/versioning commit through it.
            VaulterMediaServiceProvider::class,
            // This package binds the Drive behaviour: StorageManager, the DriveHandle
            // factory, the resumable-upload lifecycle, the PurgeTrash/SweepUploads
            // commands, the drive routes and the Drive-event Activity/outbox subscribers.
            VaulterDriveServiceProvider::class,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param Application $app
     */
    protected function defineEnvironment($app): void
    {
        $app['config']->set('database.default', 'testing');
        $app['config']->set('database.connections.testing', $this->suiteDatabaseConnection());

        // The package's `vaulter-public`/`vaulter-uploads` named rate limiters
        // (P1.3 / F-P1.8-01) are backed by the Laravel cache. Testbench's own
        // default cache store is 'database', which needs a `cache` table this
        // suite's migrations never create — pin the in-memory 'array' store so a
        // throttled route never 500s on a missing table.
        $app['config']->set('cache.default', 'array');

        // Drop the route middleware group so tests do not depend on the host's
        // 'api' group definition; authorization is exercised via the resolver.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.middleware', []);

        // The Drive suite runs OPEN under the env-gated NullRoleDriver (non-prod
        // only, H.2 / D6″): every actor gets full access so the tests exercise pure
        // file/folder/upload mechanics without an access-control lane.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.drivers.permission', 'null');

        // Pin the auto-increment strategy to bigint — the Drive tests assert integer
        // keys on the Storage→Node→File→Version chain they build.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.ids.default', 'bigint');
    }

    protected function defineDatabaseMigrations(): void
    {
        // The whole v_* schema (incl. v_upload_sessions/v_upload_parts) is owned by
        // core; load its migrations through the path-repo symlink Composer places in
        // this package's vendor dir.
        $this->loadMigrationsFrom(__DIR__.'/../vendor/axioma-studio/vaulter-core/database/migrations');
    }

    /**
     * The connection the whole suite runs on. Default: in-memory SQLite. Opt INTO
     * Postgres by exporting DB_CONNECTION=pgsql (plus the DB_* coordinates): the
     * dedicated pgsql CI lane exercises the real advisory-xact-lock / FOR UPDATE /
     * SERIALIZABLE critical sections behind the race-free upload-commit that SQLite
     * cannot model — every such test SKIPS unless the live driver is pgsql.
     *
     * @return array<string, string|bool|null>
     */
    protected function suiteDatabaseConnection(): array
    {
        if (env('DB_CONNECTION') !== 'pgsql') {
            return [
                'driver' => 'sqlite',
                'database' => ':memory:',
                'prefix' => '',
            ];
        }

        return [
            'driver' => 'pgsql',
            'host' => (string) env('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1'),
            'port' => (string) env('DB_PORT', '5432'),
            'database' => (string) env('DB_DATABASE', 'vaulter_test'),
            'username' => (string) env('DB_USERNAME', 'vaulter'),
            'password' => (string) env('DB_PASSWORD', ''),
            'charset' => 'utf8',
            'prefix' => '',
            'prefix_indexes' => true,
            'search_path' => 'public',
            'sslmode' => 'prefer',
        ];
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/BlobGarbageCollector.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Collection;
use Illuminate\Database\QueryException;
use Throwable;
use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * Two-phase, race-safe blob GC (H.3). It is ACL-blind — liveness ≠ the viewer's
 * rights, so a node a user cannot see still pins its blob; GC runs as the system
 * with no panel/workspace context.
 *
 *  PHASE 1 (mark): scan committed ∧ ref_count(cache)=0 (reconciliation rebuilds
 *  that cache from the edge table, so a blob qualifies once its last edge is
 *  detached and reconciled), then under the per-(scope,sha256) lock RE-VERIFY no
 *  live edge exists in v_file_version_blobs before tombstoning (grace deadline =
 *  now + grace, armed here so the sweep cannot delete within the grace window).
 *
 *  PHASE 2 (sweep): take tombstoned blobs past grace, RE-VERIFY under the lock
 *  (a resurrection may have flipped them back to committed), → deleting →
 *  physical delete (scope-qualified key, no cross-scope deletion) → deleted
 *  (idempotent tombstone). On PostgreSQL the sweep runs SERIALIZABLE with
 *  retry-on-40001.
 *
 * The advisory lock + UNIQUE (scope, sha256) + resurrection are what make this
 * safe against a concurrent upload-commit: whichever transaction wins the lock
 * acts on rows the other will then re-read; a commit resurrects before a sweep
 * can delete, a sweep that wins re-checks edges before deleting.
 */
final readonly class BlobGarbageCollector
{
    private const int BATCH_SIZE = 1000;

    private const int MAX_SERIALIZATION_RETRIES = 5;

    public function __construct(
        private ContentStore $content,
        private BlobLock $lock,
        private BlobStoreRepository $blobs,
        private FileVersionBlobStoreRepository $edges,
        private MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Run both phases and return a per-phase tally.
     *
     * @return array{tombstoned: int, swept: int, skipped: int}
     */
    public function collect(int $graceHours = 0): array
    {
        $grace = Config::gcGraceHours($graceHours);

        $mark = $this->mark(graceHours: $grace);
        $sweep = $this->sweep();

        return [
            'tombstoned' => $mark['tombstoned'],
            'swept' => $sweep['swept'],
            'skipped' => $mark['skipped'] + $sweep['skipped'],
        ];
    }

    /**
     * PHASE 1 — tombstone committed blobs that are genuinely unreferenced.
     *
     * @return array{tombstoned: int, skipped: int}
     */
    public function mark(int $graceHours): array
    {
        $tombstoned = 0;
        $skipped = 0;

        $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_gc_duration_seconds',
            operation: function () use ($graceHours, &$tombstoned, &$skipped): void {
                foreach ($this->candidateBatches(static fn (Builder $q): Builder => $q->garbageCollectable()) as $batch) {
                    $marked = $this->markBatch($batch, $graceHours);
                    $tombstoned += $marked;
                    $skipped += $batch->count() - $marked;

                    $this->metrics->histogram(name: 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows', value: $batch->count());
                    $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_gc_rows_scanned_total', amount: $batch->count());
                }
            },
            labels: ['phase' => 'mark'],
        );

        return ['tombstoned' => $tombstoned, 'skipped' => $skipped];
    }

    /**
     * PHASE 2 — sweep tombstoned blobs past their grace and physically delete.
     *
     * @return array{swept: int, skipped: int}
     */
    public function sweep(): array
    {
        $swept = 0;
        $skipped = 0;
        $hadBatch = false;

        $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_gc_duration_seconds',
            operation: function () use (&$swept, &$skipped, &$hadBatch): void {
                foreach ($this->candidateBatches(static fn (Builder $q): Builder => $q->sweepable()) as $batch) {
                    $hadBatch = true;
                    $batchSwept = 0;

                    foreach ($batch as $candidate) {
                        $deleted = $this->serializable(fn (): bool => $this->lockedTransaction($candidate, function (Blob $blob): bool {
                            // (the body runs inside a SERIALIZABLE transaction on PG — see
                            // serializable()/lockedTransaction(): the isolation level is set as
                            // the FIRST statement inside the transaction, before the lock.)
                            // A resurrection between scan and lock flips it back to committed —
                            // never delete then.
                            if ($blob->status !== BlobStatus::Tombstoned) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            if ($this->edges->hasLiveEdge($blob)) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            if ($blob->delete_after_at === null || $blob->delete_after_at->isFuture()) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            // Claim it, delete the scope-qualified object, finalise the
                            // idempotent tombstone. A missing object is fine — markDeleted is
                            // idempotent.
                            $this->blobs->markDeleting($blob);
                            $this->content->delete($blob->storage_key, $blob->disk);
                            $this->blobs->markDeleted($blob);

                            return true;
                        }, serializable: true));

                        $deleted ? $batchSwept++ : $skipped++;
                    }

                    $swept += $batchSwept;
                    $this->metrics->histogram(name: 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows', value: $batch->count());
                    $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_gc_objects_deleted_total', amount: $batchSwept);
                }
            },
            labels: ['phase' => 'sweep'],
        );

        if (!$hadBatch) {
            $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_gc_objects_deleted_total', amount: 0);
        }

        return ['swept' => $swept, 'skipped' => $skipped];
    }

    /**
     * Sweep PENDING blobs from a crashed staging (H.3): a pending row older than
     * the grace window whose commit never finished and that carries no edge. Its
     * (possibly partial) object is deleted and the row is finalised as a deleted
     * tombstone, all under the lock so a slow-but-live commit is not stolen.
     *
     * @return array{swept: int, skipped: int}
     */
    public function sweepPending(int $graceHours = 0): array
    {
        $grace = Config::gcGraceHours($graceHours);
        $cutoff = now()->subHours($grace);
        $swept = 0;
        $skipped = 0;

        $batches = $this->candidateBatches(static fn (Builder $q): Builder => $q
            ->where('status', BlobStatus::Pending->value)
            ->where('first_seen_at', '<', $cutoff));

        foreach ($batches as $batch) {
            foreach ($batch as $candidate) {
                // Symmetry with sweep() (F-P1.5-02): the pending-sweep runs its unit
                // SERIALIZABLE on PostgreSQL with retry-on-40001, so a transient
                // serialization failure under concurrent GC is retried rather than
                // propagated as a QueryException that aborts the whole pass.
                $acted = $this->serializable(fn (): bool => $this->lockedTransaction($candidate, function (Blob $blob) use ($cutoff): bool {
                    if ($blob->status !== BlobStatus::Pending) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    // A pending row that grew an edge (a commit landing right now) is
                    // live — leave it.
                    if ($this->edges->hasLiveEdge($blob)) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    if ($blob->first_seen_at !== null && $blob->first_seen_at->greaterThanOrEqualTo($cutoff)) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    $this->blobs->markDeleting($blob);
                    $this->content->delete($blob->storage_key, $blob->disk);
                    $this->blobs->markDeleted($blob);

                    return true;
                }, serializable: true));

                $acted ? $swept++ : $skipped++;
            }

            $this->metrics->histogram(name: 'vaulter_gc_batch_rows', value: $batch->count());
        }

        return ['swept' => $swept, 'skipped' => $skipped];
    }

    /**
     * Run a single-blob critical section inside a transaction whose first
     * statement is the per-(scope,sha256) lock, re-reading the row FOR UPDATE on
     * MySQL. The callback receives the locked, fresh row and returns whether it
     * acted.
     *
     * When $serializable is set on PostgreSQL the isolation level is raised as the
     * VERY FIRST statement inside the open transaction block — the only place
     * `SET TRANSACTION` takes effect on PG (issued outside a block it warns and is
     * ignored). It precedes even the advisory lock, which is allowed: SET
     * TRANSACTION only forbids a prior data statement, not a prior SET.
     *
     * The raise is skipped when this is NOT a top-level transaction (transactionLevel
     * > 1) — i.e. the GC unit runs nested inside a caller's open transaction (a host
     * wrapping the sweep, or RefreshDatabase in tests). PostgreSQL forbids changing the
     * isolation level once a transaction has issued a data statement, so SET would 25001;
     * the only valid behaviour is to inherit the enclosing transaction's isolation. The
     * advisory lock + FOR UPDATE still serialise the critical section either way.
     *
     * @param \Closure(Blob): bool $work
     */
    private function lockedTransaction(Blob $candidate, \Closure $work, bool $serializable = false): bool
    {
        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($candidate, $work, $serializable): bool {
            if ($serializable && $this->lock->isPostgres() && $this->database->connection()->transactionLevel() <= 1) {
                $this->database->connection()->statement('SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL SERIALIZABLE');
            }

            $this->lock->acquire(scope: $this->scopeFor($candidate), sha256: $candidate->sha256);

            $fresh = $this->blobs->lockForUpdate($candidate);

            return $work($fresh);
        });
    }

    /**
     * On PostgreSQL run the unit SERIALIZABLE and retry on a 40001 serialization
     * failure (H.3) — the isolation level itself is raised INSIDE the transaction
     * (see lockedTransaction), this only drives the retry loop. On MySQL/SQLite the
     * FOR-UPDATE/single-writer path already serialises, so run once.
     *
     * @param \Closure(): bool $unit
     */
    private function serializable(\Closure $unit): bool
    {
        if (!$this->lock->isPostgres()) {
            return $unit();
        }

        $attempt = 0;

        while (true) {
            try {
                return $unit();
            } catch (QueryException $exception) {
                $attempt++;

                if (!$this->isSerializationFailure($exception) || $attempt >= self::MAX_SERIALIZATION_RETRIES) {
                    throw $exception;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private function isSerializationFailure(Throwable $exception): bool
    {
        // PostgreSQL serialization_failure → SQLSTATE 40001.
        return str_contains($exception->getMessage(), '40001');
    }

    /**
     * The scope discriminator for a blob's advisory lock — the same segment baked
     * into its storage_key, so GC and commit lock the same liveness unit.
     */
    private function scopeFor(Blob $blob): string
    {
        return $blob->scope_key;
    }

    /**
     * Lock before reading state/liveness, but amortise the transaction and reads
     * over a bounded batch. Keep repository writes so model events still fire.
     *
     * @param Collection<int, Blob> $batch
     */
    private function markBatch(Collection $batch, int $graceHours): int
    {
        $model = Config::blobModel();

        // Custom models/observers may change another candidate while saving.
        // Preserve the per-row fresh liveness check for those extension points.
        if ($model !== Blob::class || $this->hasBlobListeners()) {
            $marked = 0;

            foreach ($batch as $candidate) {
                $acted = $this->lockedTransaction($candidate, function (Blob $blob) use ($graceHours): bool {
                    if ($blob->status !== BlobStatus::Committed || $this->edges->hasLiveEdge($blob)) {
                        return false;
                    }

                    $this->blobs->tombstone(blob: $blob, graceHours: $graceHours);

                    return true;
                });

                $marked += (int) $acted;
            }

            return $marked;
        }

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($batch, $graceHours): int {
            if ($this->lock->isPostgres()) {
                $keys = array_values($batch->map(fn (Blob $blob): string => 'cas:'.$this->scopeFor($blob).':'.$blob->sha256)->all());

                $this->lock->acquireMany($keys);
            }

            $model = Config::blobModel();
            $query = $model::query()->whereKey($batch->modelKeys())->orderBy((new $model)->getKeyName());

            if ($this->lock->usesRowLock()) {
                $query->forceIndex('PRIMARY')->lockForUpdate();
            }

            $fresh = $query->get();
            $edgeModel = Config::fileVersionBlobModel();
            /** @var \Illuminate\Support\Collection<int, int|string> $liveIds */
            $liveIds = $edgeModel::query()->whereIn('blob_id', $batch->modelKeys())->distinct()->pluck('blob_id');
            $live = array_fill_keys($liveIds->all(), true);
            $marked = 0;

            foreach ($fresh as $blob) {
                if ($blob->status !== BlobStatus::Committed) {
                    continue;
                }

                if (isset($live[$blob->id])) {
                    continue;
                }

                $this->blobs->tombstone(blob: $blob, graceHours: $graceHours);
                $marked++;
            }

            return $marked;
        });
    }

    private function hasBlobListeners(): bool
    {
        $dispatcher = Blob::getEventDispatcher();

        return array_any(
            ['retrieved', 'saving', 'saved', 'updating', 'updated'],
            static fn (string $event): bool => $dispatcher?->hasListeners('eloquent.'.$event.': '.Blob::class) ?? false,
        );
    }

    /**
     * Scan by key, never offset: processed and skipped rows both advance the
     * checkpoint even though status changes remove rows from the candidate set.
     * A phase-start high-water key keeps inserts from extending a pass forever.
     * Queries and materialised batches hold at most BATCH_SIZE rows, as the
     * system (no panel/workspace — GC is ACL-blind).
     *
     * @param  \Closure(Builder<Blob>): Builder<Blob> $scope
     * @return iterable<Collection<int, Blob>>
     */
    private function candidateBatches(\Closure $scope): iterable
    {
        $model = Config::blobModel();
        $query = $scope($model::query());
        $key = $query->getModel()->getKeyName();
        $upper = (clone $query)->reorder($key, 'desc')->value($key);

        if ($upper === null) {
            return;
        }

        $last = null;

        do {
            $page = (clone $query)->where($key, '<=', $upper)->reorder($key)->limit(self::BATCH_SIZE);

            if ($last !== null) {
                $page->where($key, '>', $last);
            }

            $batch = $page->get();

            if ($batch->isEmpty()) {
                return;
            }

            $last = $batch->last()->getKey();

            yield $batch;
        } while ($batch->count() === self::BATCH_SIZE);
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/BlobReconciler.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Observability\MetricsRecorder;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\Support\Identity;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Blob reconciliation (H.3): ref_count is a CACHE, not the truth, so it drifts and
 * must be rebuilt from the edge table — the real liveness source. Reconciliation
 * also surfaces the two integrity hazards a CAS must watch, the inverse images of
 * each other:
 *
 *  - DANGLING ROW   — a committed blob row whose canonical object is missing on
 *    disk (a half-promoted commit, a botched manual delete). Found by reconcile()
 *    walking the ROW side.
 *  - ORPHAN OBJECT  — bytes on disk with NO blob row at all (a commit promoted its
 *    bytes then its transaction rolled the row back; or leaked bytes). Found by
 *    sweepOrphans() walking the DISK side.
 *
 * reconcile() never deletes — it rewrites the ref_count cache and reports.
 * sweepOrphans() is report-only by default; with $prune it physically deletes true
 * orphans under the SAME per-(scope,sha256) lock + grace discipline the GC uses, so
 * a concurrent commit can never have its in-flight bytes swept out from under it.
 *
 * Two capability gates make the destructive path safe and BC-clean:
 *  - LISTING. The disk-walk needs the CAS driver to enumerate the disk. That seam
 *    lives on the optional {@see ListableContentStore} capability (NOT the base
 *    ContentStore contract — adding to it would break every external driver). When
 *    the active store is not listable, sweepOrphans() refuses cleanly (reports
 *    listingSupported=false) instead of failing; this also surfaces the S3 /
 *    non-FilesystemAdapter "cannot enumerate" case rather than silently skipping.
 *  - PRUNE SERIALISATION. The prune re-reads under the lock then deletes BYTES with
 *    NO row write, so it is ordered against a concurrent commit ONLY on drivers
 *    whose lock serialises a write-less section (PG advisory xact lock; MySQL FOR
 *    UPDATE). On SQLite that ordering never engages (acquire() is a no-op, FOR
 *    UPDATE is ignored) so a concurrent commit could INSERT the owning row between
 *    the recheck and the delete and we would delete LIVE bytes — therefore prune is
 *    REFUSED on SQLite (pruneSupported=false): detection still runs, deletion does
 *    not. Report-only works on EVERY driver. (The GC is immune: it writes a real
 *    markDeleting() that forces SQLite's write lock; the prune deliberately writes
 *    nothing, so it cannot lean on that.)
 */
final readonly class BlobReconciler
{
    /** Both historical two-shard keys and lossless single-shard keys. */
    private const string KEY_PATTERN = '#^(?<scope>global|[ts](?:\d+|null|[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26})|g|[td]-~?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128})/[0-9a-f]{2}/(?:[0-9a-f]{2}/)?(?<sha256>[0-9a-f]{64})$#';

    public function __construct(
        private ContentStore $content,
        private BlobStoreRepository $blobs,
        private FileVersionBlobStoreRepository $edges,
        private BlobLock $lock,
        private MetricsRecorder $metrics,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * Rebuild ref_count for every blob from its live edge count and collect the
     * dangling rows. Returns a report tally.
     *
     * @return array{reconciled: int, changed: int, dangling: list<string>}
     */
    public function reconcile(): array
    {
        $reconciled = 0;
        $changed = 0;
        $dangling = [];

        $this->metrics->timing(
            name: 'vaulter_reconcile_duration_seconds',
            operation: function () use (&$reconciled, &$changed, &$dangling): void {
                foreach ($this->blobModel()::query()->cursor() as $blob) {
                    /** @var Blob $blob */
                    $reconciled++;

                    $count = $this->edges->countEdges($blob);

                    if ($blob->ref_count !== $count) {
                        $this->blobs->setRefCount(blob: $blob, count: $count);
                        $changed++;
                    }

                    // A committed blob must have its bytes on disk; a missing object is a
                    // dangling row the operator should investigate (re-upload or purge).
                    if ($blob->status === BlobStatus::Committed && !$this->content->exists($blob->storage_key, $blob->disk)) {
                        $dangling[] = Identity::stringify($blob->getKey());
                    }
                }
            },
        );

        if ($reconciled > 0) {
            $this->metrics->counter(name: 'vaulter_reconcile_objects_total', amount: $reconciled);
        }

        return [
            'reconciled' => $reconciled,
            'changed' => $changed,
            'dangling' => $dangling,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Walk the DISK for orphan objects — bytes that match the committed CAS key
     * layout but have NO blob row — and, when $prune AND the driver serialises the
     * write-less prune section, physically delete the true orphans (H.3).
     * Report-only by default (a safe dry run on EVERY driver).
     *
     * CAPABILITY GATES:
     *  - If the active store cannot enumerate the disk (not a {@see ListableContentStore},
     *    e.g. a streaming-only or non-FilesystemAdapter driver) the walk is REFUSED:
     *    returns listingSupported=false, scanned 0, no deletion — the command reports
     *    "listing unsupported", not a fatal error.
     *  - If $prune is requested but the driver does NOT serialise a write-less
     *    critical section (SQLite — see {@see BlobLock::serializesCriticalSection()}),
     *    pruning is REFUSED: detection still runs and orphans are reported, but
     *    pruneSupported=false and nothing is deleted (deleting under SQLite would
     *    race a concurrent commit and could destroy live bytes). Run report-only, or
     *    prune on the production PostgreSQL/MySQL.
     *
     * SAFETY (this deletes bytes when it does prune):
     *  1. A path is a candidate ONLY if it EXACTLY matches the committed key layout
     *     (KEY_PATTERN) AND keyFor(scope, sha256) re-derives the SAME path — so the
     *     shard bytes are the real shard of that sha256. Staging/tmp, resumable
     *     parts and foreign files never match and are skipped untouched.
     *  2. A matching path with ANY blob row (looked up DISK-AGNOSTICALLY, since
     *     storage_key is globally unique) is owned, not an orphan.
     *  3. A row-less object is a deletable orphan only once it is OLDER than the
     *     orphan grace — a just-promoted object may be a commit whose row is not yet
     *     durable; a fresher one is skipped.
     *  4. Pruning happens under the per-(scope,sha256) lock inside a transaction,
     *     RE-checking both the row-absence (under the MySQL row lock) and the grace
     *     AFTER taking the lock (a concurrent commit may have just created the row /
     *     re-touched the bytes), then deletes idempotently.
     *
     * @return array{scanned: int, orphans: list<string>, pruned: int, skipped: int, listingSupported: bool, pruneSupported: bool}
     */
    public function sweepOrphans(?string $disk = null, int $graceHours = 0, bool $prune = false): array
    {
        // The disk-walk seam is an OPTIONAL capability, not the base ContentStore
        // contract: a non-listable store cannot be walked, so refuse cleanly.
        $content = $this->content;

        if (!$content instanceof ListableContentStore) {
            return $this->unsupportedListing();
        }

        $disk ??= Config::defaultDisk();
        $grace = Config::orphanGraceHours($graceHours);

        // The prune deletes bytes with NO row write, so it is only safe on a driver
        // whose lock serialises a write-less section. On SQLite we still DETECT and
        // REPORT, but never delete.
        $pruneSupported = $this->lock->serializesCriticalSection();
        $doPrune = $prune && $pruneSupported;

        $scanned = 0;
        $orphans = [];
        $pruned = 0;
        $skipped = 0;

        foreach ($content->listKeys($disk) as $path) {
            $scanned++;

            $parsed = $this->parseManagedKey($path);

            // Not a committed CAS object (staging/tmp, resumable part, foreign file,
            // or a mis-sharded path that does not round-trip) — never touch it.
            if ($parsed === null) {
                $skipped++;

                continue;
            }

            // An object owned by a blob row (any status, ANY disk — storage_key is
            // globally unique) is not an orphan.
            if ($this->blobs->findByStorageKey(storageKey: $path) !== null) {
                $skipped++;

                continue;
            }

            // A row-less object younger than the grace may be a commit-in-progress
            // whose row is not yet durable — leave it for a later pass.
            if (!$this->isPastGrace(content: $content, path: $path, disk: $disk, graceHours: $grace)) {
                $skipped++;

                continue;
            }

            $orphans[] = $path;

            if (!$doPrune) {
                continue;
            }

            $this->pruneOrphan(content: $content, path: $path, disk: $disk, scope: $parsed['scope'], sha256: $parsed['sha256'], graceHours: $grace)
                ? $pruned++
                : $skipped++;
        }

        return [
            'scanned' => $scanned,
            'orphans' => $orphans,
            'pruned' => $pruned,
            'skipped' => $skipped,
            'listingSupported' => true,
            'pruneSupported' => $pruneSupported,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * The walk-refused result for a store that cannot enumerate the disk: nothing
     * scanned, nothing pruned, both capabilities reported off.
     *
     * @return array{scanned: int, orphans: list<string>, pruned: int, skipped: int, listingSupported: bool, pruneSupported: bool}
     */
    private function unsupportedListing(): array
    {
        return [
            'scanned' => 0,
            'orphans' => [],
            'pruned' => 0,
            'skipped' => 0,
            'listingSupported' => false,
            'pruneSupported' => false,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * Delete one orphan under the per-(scope,sha256) advisory lock (mirrors
     * BlobGarbageCollector::lockedTransaction): take the lock as the first statement,
     * then RE-CHECK that no row appeared and the object is still past grace before
     * the idempotent physical delete. Returns whether the object was deleted (false
     * ⇒ a concurrent commit won the race / the bytes were re-touched / already gone).
     *
     * Only ever reached on a driver whose lock serialises this write-less section
     * (sweepOrphans gates on {@see BlobLock::serializesCriticalSection()}); the
     * row-absence recheck takes the MySQL row lock so a concurrent INSERT contends.
     *
     * @param ContentStore&ListableContentStore $content the active store, already
     *                                                   confirmed listable by the caller so its age probe is usable here
     */
    private function pruneOrphan(ContentStore&ListableContentStore $content, string $path, string $disk, string $scope, string $sha256, int $graceHours): bool
    {
        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($content, $path, $disk, $scope, $sha256, $graceHours): bool {
            $this->lock->acquire(scope: $scope, sha256: $sha256);

            // A commit that won the lock between the cheap scan and here has now
            // created the row — the bytes are owned, never delete them. Disk-agnostic
            // (storage_key is globally unique); lock: true takes the MySQL row lock.
            if ($this->blobs->findByStorageKey(storageKey: $path, lock: true) !== null) {
                return false;
            }

            // The same commit may have re-promoted (re-touched) the bytes; re-apply
            // the grace under the lock so a fresh object is never swept.
            if (!$this->isPastGrace(content: $content, path: $path, disk: $disk, graceHours: $graceHours)) {
                return false;
            }

            // Idempotent: a missing object is fine.
            $content->delete($path, $disk);

            return true;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Whether the object is older than the orphan grace. A null mtime (absent or the
     * disk cannot report it) is treated as NOT past grace, so an object whose age we
     * cannot establish is skipped, never deleted.
     */
    private function isPastGrace(ContentStore&ListableContentStore $content, string $path, string $disk, int $graceHours): bool
    {
        $lastModified = $content->lastModified($path, $disk);

        if ($lastModified === null) {
            return false;
        }

        return $lastModified < now()->subHours($graceHours)->getTimestamp();
    }

    /**
     * Classify a disk path as a committed CAS object and extract its (scope, sha256),
     * or null when it is not one. The match is exact AND verified by round-tripping
     * keyFor(scope, sha256) === $path, so the shard bytes in the path are guaranteed
     * to be the real shard of that sha256 (a mis-sharded or scope-spoofed path is
     * rejected). This is the primary guard that the sweep only ever touches genuine
     * canonical objects.
     *
     * @return array{scope: string, sha256: string}|null
     */
    private function parseManagedKey(string $path): ?array
    {
        if (preg_match(self::KEY_PATTERN, $path, $matches) !== 1) {
            return null;
        }

        try {
            $scope = BlobScope::fromSegment($matches['scope']);
        } catch (\InvalidArgumentException) {
            return null;
        }
        $sha256 = $matches['sha256'];

        // Re-derive the canonical key from the parsed parts; the shard bytes must be
        // the real shard of this sha256 for the path to round-trip.
        if ($this->content->keyFor(scope: $scope, sha256: $sha256) !== $path) {
            return null;
        }

        return ['scope' => $scope->key(), 'sha256' => $sha256];
    }

    /**
     * @return class-string<Blob>
     */
    private function blobModel(): string
    {
        return Config::blobModel();
    }
}

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/BlobStore.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore as BlobStoreContract;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Models\Drive\FileVersion;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Stage and hash outside the transaction, then lock (scope_key, sha256) before
 * row lookup, resurrection and promotion. SQL rollback can leave orphan bytes;
 * the reconciler uses this same lock before collecting them.
 */
final readonly class BlobStore implements BlobStoreContract
{
    public function __construct(
        private ContentStore $content,
        private BlobLock $lock,
        private BlobStoreRepository $blobs,
        private FileVersionBlobStoreRepository $edges,
        private VaulterDatabase $database,
    ) {}

    /**
     * @param resource $stream
     */
    public function commit(mixed $stream, BlobScope $scope, ?string $disk = null): Blob
    {
        $staged = $this->content->stage($stream);

        try {
            return $this->commitStaged($staged, $scope, $disk);
        } finally {
            $staged->discard();
        }
    }

    public function commitStaged(StagedObject $staged, BlobScope $scope, ?string $disk = null): Blob
    {
        $disk ??= Config::defaultDisk();
        $storageKey = $this->content->keyFor(scope: $scope, sha256: $staged->sha256());

        return $this->database->transaction(function () use ($staged, $scope, $disk, $storageKey): Blob {
            $this->lock->acquire(scope: $scope->key(), sha256: $staged->sha256());

            $result = $this->blobs->findOrCreate(
                sha256: $staged->sha256(),
                sizeBytes: $staged->sizeBytes(),
                storageKey: $storageKey,
                disk: $disk,
                scope: $scope,
                tenantId: $scope->tenantId(),
                storageId: $scope->storageId(),
            );

            // Promotion is idempotent; a rollback leaves bytes for reconciliation.
            if ($result['created'] || !$this->content->exists($result['blob']->storage_key, $result['blob']->disk)) {
                $this->content->promote(staged: $staged, key: $result['blob']->storage_key, disk: $result['blob']->disk);
            }

            return $result['blob'];
        });
    }

    /**
     * @param array{role?: string, ordinal?: int, offset_bytes?: int|null, length_bytes?: int|null} $attributes
     */
    public function attach(FileVersion $version, Blob $blob, array $attributes = []): void
    {
        $this->edges->attach(version: $version, blob: $blob, attributes: $attributes);
    }

    public function detach(FileVersion $version, Blob $blob, string $role = 'data', int $ordinal = 0): void
    {
        $this->edges->detach(version: $version, blob: $blob, role: $role, ordinal: $ordinal);
    }

    /**
     * @return resource|null
     */
    public function read(Blob $blob): mixed
    {
        return $this->content->get($blob->storage_key, $blob->disk);
    }
}
