# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/FlysystemStorageDriver.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use DateTimeInterface;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Filesystem\Filesystem;
use Illuminate\Filesystem\FilesystemAdapter;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use League\Flysystem\UnableToRetrieveMetadata;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Support\Config;

/**
 * The default StorageDriver (H.3): a thin Flysystem-disk abstraction. The disk is
 * resolved per call (a blob's v_storages.disk, else the install default); writes
 * are stream writes; the conditional-create / object-store checksum specifics are
 * applied only when the underlying disk is an object store, degrading gracefully
 * to a best-effort exists() guard on the local / sqlite-test disk.
 */
final class FlysystemStorageDriver implements ListableStorageDriver, StorageDriver
{
    public function disk(?string $disk = null): Filesystem
    {
        return Storage::disk($disk ?? Config::defaultDisk());
    }

    /**
     * @param resource             $stream
     * @param array<string, mixed> $options
     */
    public function put(string $key, mixed $stream, ?string $disk = null, bool $ifNoneMatch = false, array $options = []): bool
    {
        $filesystem = $this->disk($disk);

        // Conditional create. On an object store this maps to If-None-Match: * so
        // a concurrent uploader of identical bytes cannot clobber; we surface the
        // "already there" race as a false return (re-SELECT + reuse, H.3). On a
        // local/sqlite test disk there is no conditional PUT, so we degrade to a
        // best-effort exists() guard — the per-(scope,sha256) lock in BlobStore is
        // the real serialiser there.
        if ($ifNoneMatch && $filesystem->exists($key)) {
            return false;
        }

        // Object stores land a write ATOMICALLY server-side: a failed/partial PUT
        // never appears under the key, so there is no truncated-object window to
        // guard — and a sibling temp + move there would only double the transfer
        // and, past the S3 5 GiB single-request CopyObject limit, make the move FAIL
        // where a direct PUT succeeds. Write straight to the canonical key on those
        // disks; reserve temp-then-move for non-atomic disks (local/ftp) where a
        // crash mid-write WOULD leave a truncated object under the canonical CAS key
        // (F-P1.5-01; review def.2).
        if ($this->writesAtomically($filesystem)) {
            // x-amz-checksum-sha256 (in $options) lets S3 verify the bytes server-side.
            $written = $filesystem->writeStream($key, $stream, $options);

            return $written !== false;
        }

        return $this->promoteViaTemp($filesystem, $key, $stream, $ifNoneMatch, $options);
    }

    /**
     * Temp-then-atomic-move promotion for a NON-atomic disk (F-P1.5-01): a crash
     * mid-write must never leave a TRUNCATED object under the canonical CAS key (a
     * partial object there would be trusted as committed — silent CAS corruption). We
     * stream into a sibling temp key and only atomically move it onto the canonical
     * key once the whole object landed; ANY failure — writeStream false/throw OR move
     * false/throw — reclaims the temp and never leaves a partial under the canonical
     * key (review def.1 + def.2). The temp is a sibling (same directory ⇒ same
     * filesystem) so the move is a rename on the local disk.
     *
     * @param resource             $stream
     * @param array<string, mixed> $options
     */
    private function promoteViaTemp(Filesystem $filesystem, string $key, mixed $stream, bool $ifNoneMatch, array $options): bool
    {
        $tempKey = $this->tempKeyFor($key);

        try {
            $written = $filesystem->writeStream($tempKey, $stream, $options);

            if ($written === false) {
                $this->deleteQuietly($filesystem, $tempKey);

                return false;
            }
        } catch (\Throwable $exception) {
            // Reclaim the partial temp; the canonical key was never written.
            $this->deleteQuietly($filesystem, $tempKey);

            throw $exception;
        }

        // Conditional-create only (CAS promote, $ifNoneMatch): a concurrent uploader
        // of the SAME content-address may have promoted its (byte-identical) object
        // first, so drop our temp and treat it as a dedup hit rather than clobber it.
        // The plain overwrite path (e.g. an S3 part re-upload REPLACING its bytes)
        // must fall through and move over the existing key.
        if ($ifNoneMatch && $filesystem->exists($key)) {
            $this->deleteQuietly($filesystem, $tempKey);

            return true;
        }

        // Atomic promotion: a rename on the local disk (same directory ⇒ same
        // filesystem), overwriting any existing object for the overwrite path. move()
        // returns FALSE on a non-throwing disk and THROWS UnableToMoveFile on a
        // throwing one; a leaked temp on either path would linger under a sibling key,
        // so reclaim it and report failure both ways rather than falsely returning
        // success while the canonical key was never written (review def.1 + def.2).
        try {
            $moved = $filesystem->move($tempKey, $key);
        } catch (\Throwable $exception) {
            $this->deleteQuietly($filesystem, $tempKey);

            throw $exception;
        }

        if ($moved === false) {
            $this->deleteQuietly($filesystem, $tempKey);

            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Whether the disk lands a write atomically (F-P1.5-01 / review def.2). An object
     * store commits a PUT server-side — a failed upload never appears under the key —
     * so it needs no temp-then-move staging. A local/ftp disk, and any disk we cannot
     * positively identify as an object store, is treated as non-atomic so the
     * truncated-object guard still applies. We reuse the same object-store signal this
     * driver already trusts for signed URLs (temporaryUrl()): providesTemporaryUrls().
     */
    private function writesAtomically(Filesystem $filesystem): bool
    {
        return $filesystem instanceof FilesystemAdapter && $filesystem->providesTemporaryUrls();
    }

    /**
     * A sibling temp key for the atomic-move promotion (F-P1.5-01): same directory
     * as the canonical key (so the move is a same-filesystem rename on local) with
     * a random suffix so two concurrent promotions never collide on the temp.
     */
    private function tempKeyFor(string $key): string
    {
        return $key.'.tmp-'.bin2hex(random_bytes(8));
    }

    private function deleteQuietly(Filesystem $filesystem, string $key): void
    {
        if ($filesystem->exists($key)) {
            $filesystem->delete($key);
        }
    }

    /**
     * @return resource|null
     */
    public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
    {
        $filesystem = $this->disk($disk);

        if (!$filesystem->exists($key)) {
            return null;
        }

        $stream = $filesystem->readStream($key);

        return is_resource($stream) ? $stream : null;
    }

    public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
    {
        return $this->disk($disk)->exists($key);
    }

    public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $filesystem = $this->disk($disk);

        if ($filesystem->exists($key)) {
            $filesystem->delete($key);
        }
    }

    public function lastModified(string $key, ?string $disk = null): ?int
    {
        $filesystem = $this->disk($disk);

        if (!$filesystem->exists($key)) {
            return null;
        }

        // The Flysystem-backed adapter returns the mtime as epoch seconds; a disk
        // that cannot report it throws, which we swallow to a null (treat as
        // "age unknown" → the caller skips rather than deletes).
        try {
            return $filesystem->lastModified($key);
        } catch (UnableToRetrieveMetadata) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * @return iterable<string>
     */
    public function listContents(?string $disk = null): iterable
    {
        $filesystem = $this->disk($disk);

        // The recursive listing lives on the wrapped Flysystem operator, not the
        // Laravel contract; a disk whose adapter we cannot reach yields nothing
        // rather than throwing (the orphan walk then finds no candidates there).
        if (!$filesystem instanceof FilesystemAdapter) {
            return;
        }

        foreach ($filesystem->getDriver()->listContents('', deep: true) as $attributes) {
            if ($attributes->isFile()) {
                yield $attributes->path();
            }
        }
    }

    public function temporaryUrl(string $key, DateTimeInterface $expiresAt, ?string $disk = null): ?string
    {
        $filesystem = $this->disk($disk);

        // Only object stores that opted in can mint a signed URL; the local /
        // sqlite-test disk returns null rather than throwing.
        if (!$filesystem instanceof FilesystemAdapter || !$filesystem->providesTemporaryUrls()) {
            return null;
        }

        return $filesystem->temporaryUrl($key, $expiresAt);
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/Sha256ContentStore.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use RuntimeException;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ListableStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StorageDriver;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * The default ContentStore (H.3): hash the LOCAL staged temp file, never the live
 * stream. An incoming stream is drained into a seekable temp file; the sha256 is
 * computed from those durable bytes (a lying client sha256 or an S3 multipart
 * ETag is irrelevant). Promotion writes the staged bytes to the canonical
 * scope-qualified key with a conditional create so a concurrent identical upload
 * cannot clobber, and on S3 attaches x-amz-checksum-sha256 for server-side
 * verification (config-aware; ignored by the local/sqlite test disk).
 */
final readonly class Sha256ContentStore implements ContentStore, ListableContentStore
{
    /**
     * Read buffer for streaming the upload into the staged temp file.
     */
    private const int CHUNK_BYTES = 1_048_576;

    public function __construct(
        private readonly StorageDriver $driver,
    ) {}

    /**
     * @param resource $stream
     */
    public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
    {
        if (!is_resource($stream)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('ContentStore::stage() expects an open stream resource.');
        }

        $path = $this->newTempPath();
        $target = @fopen($path, 'wb');

        if ($target === false) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Unable to open staging temp file [{$path}].");
        }

        $hash = hash_init('sha256');
        $size = 0;

        // Rewind the source where possible so a partially consumed stream still
        // stages whole; non-seekable streams (php://input) are read as-is.
        if (stream_get_meta_data($stream)['seekable']) {
            @rewind($stream);
        }

        while (!feof($stream)) {
            $buffer = fread($stream, self::CHUNK_BYTES);

            if ($buffer === false) {
                break;
            }

            hash_update($hash, $buffer);
            $size += strlen($buffer);
            fwrite($target, $buffer);
        }

        fclose($target);

        return new StagedTempObject(
            path: $path,
            sha256: hash_final($hash),
            sizeBytes: $size,
        );
    }

    public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        // Already at the canonical key (a dedup hit promoted by a concurrent
        // uploader, or an idempotent re-commit) — nothing to write.
        if ($this->driver->exists($key, $disk)) {
            return;
        }

        $stream = $staged->readStream();

        try {
            // If-None-Match: * — a 412/409 means we were overtaken; the driver
            // returns false and we treat that as "already there" (H.3), not fatal.
            $this->driver->put(
                key: $key,
                stream: $stream,
                disk: $disk,
                ifNoneMatch: true,
                options: ['ChecksumSHA256' => $this->base64Sha256($staged->sha256())],
            );
        } finally {
            if (is_resource($stream)) {
                fclose($stream);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * @return resource|null
     */
    public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
    {
        return $this->driver->get($key, $disk);
    }

    public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
    {
        return $this->driver->exists($key, $disk);
    }

    public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
    {
        $this->driver->delete($key, $disk);
    }

    public function lastModified(string $key, ?string $disk = null): ?int
    {
        // The listing capability lives on the DRIVER, not the base StorageDriver
        // contract; a non-listable driver cannot report age, so we degrade to null
        // (age unknown ⇒ the orphan walk skips rather than deletes).
        if (!$this->driver instanceof ListableStorageDriver) {
            return null;
        }

        return $this->driver->lastModified($key, $disk);
    }

    /**
     * @return iterable<string>
     */
    public function listKeys(?string $disk = null): iterable
    {
        // Only delegate when the underlying driver can actually enumerate the disk;
        // otherwise yield nothing so this store reports no objects rather than
        // calling a method the base StorageDriver contract no longer carries.
        if (!$this->driver instanceof ListableStorageDriver) {
            return;
        }

        yield from $this->driver->listContents($disk);
    }

    public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
    {
        $segment = $scope->segment();
        $a = substr($sha256, 0, 2);
        $legacyShard = $scope->isLegacy() ? substr($sha256, 2, 2).'/' : '';

        return "{$segment}/{$a}/{$legacyShard}{$sha256}";
    }

    /**
     * S3 expects the checksum as base64 of the raw digest, not the hex string.
     */
    private function base64Sha256(string $hexSha256): string
    {
        $raw = hex2bin($hexSha256);

        return $raw === false ? '' : base64_encode($raw);
    }

    private function newTempPath(): string
    {
        $path = tempnam(sys_get_temp_dir(), 'vaulter_cas_');

        if ($path === false) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Unable to allocate a staging temp file.');
        }

        return $path;
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/src/Cas/StagedTempObject.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Cas;

use RuntimeException;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;

/**
 * A StagedObject backed by a local seekable temp file (H.3). The sha256 was
 * recomputed from THESE durable bytes by the ContentStore, so promotion and the
 * dedup decision never trust the live stream or a client hash. discard() removes
 * the temp file and is idempotent.
 */
final class StagedTempObject implements StagedObject
{
    private bool $discarded = false;

    public function __construct(
        private readonly string $path,
        private readonly string $sha256,
        private readonly int $sizeBytes,
    ) {}

    public function path(): string
    {
        return $this->path;
    }

    public function sha256(): string
    {
        return $this->sha256;
    }

    public function sizeBytes(): int
    {
        return $this->sizeBytes;
    }

    /**
     * @return resource
     */
    public function readStream(): mixed
    {
        if ($this->discarded) {
            throw new RuntimeException('Cannot read a discarded staged object.');
        }

        $stream = @fopen($this->path, 'rb');

        if ($stream === false) {
            throw new RuntimeException("Unable to reopen staged temp file [{$this->path}].");
        }

        return $stream;
    }

    public function discard(): void
    {
        if ($this->discarded) {
            return;
        }

        $this->discarded = true;

        if (is_file($this->path)) {
            @unlink($this->path);
        }
    }
}

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobCommitAtomicityTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Cas\BlobLock;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\StagedObject;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\FlysystemStorageDriver;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\Sha256ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Cas\BlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\Repositories\Drive\FileVersionBlobStoreRepository;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * Commit atomicity (H.3): if the transaction rolls back, no blob row is left
 * behind. The staged temp file is always discarded. (The physical object is only
 * promoted for a newly created row, so a roll back before promotion leaves the
 * disk clean too.)
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('rollback не оставляет висящей строки blob', function (): void {
    // A ContentStore whose promote() blows up mid-transaction forces a rollback
    // after the row INSERT — proving the INSERT is inside the tx and reverts.
    $exploding = new class implements ContentStore
    {
        public function __construct(private readonly ContentStore $inner = new Sha256ContentStore(new FlysystemStorageDriver)) {}

        public function stage(mixed $stream): StagedObject
        {
            return $this->inner->stage($stream);
        }

        public function promote(StagedObject $staged, string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
        {
            throw new RuntimeException('promote boom');
        }

        public function get(string $key, ?string $disk = null): mixed
        {
            return $this->inner->get($key, $disk);
        }

        public function exists(string $key, ?string $disk = null): bool
        {
            return false; // force the promote branch
        }

        public function delete(string $key, ?string $disk = null): void
        {
            $this->inner->delete($key, $disk);
        }

        public function keyFor(BlobScope $scope, string $sha256): string
        {
            return $this->inner->keyFor($scope, $sha256);
        }
    };

    $store = new BlobStore(
        content: $exploding,
        lock: app(BlobLock::class),
        blobs: app(BlobStoreRepository::class),
        edges: app(FileVersionBlobStoreRepository::class),
        database: app(VaulterDatabase::class),
    );

    expect(fn () => $store->commit(stringStream('boom bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused')))
        ->toThrow(RuntimeException::class, 'promote boom');

    // The transaction rolled back: zero rows, zero objects.
    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(0)
        ->and(Storage::disk('local')->allFiles())->toBe([]);
});

it('staged temp-файл удаляется после успешного коммита', function (): void {
    $before = glob(sys_get_temp_dir().'/vaulter_cas_*') ?: [];

    app(BlobStore::class)->commit(stringStream('temp cleanup'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    $after = glob(sys_get_temp_dir().'/vaulter_cas_*') ?: [];

    expect(count($after))->toBeLessThanOrEqual(count($before));
});

function stringStream(string $bytes)
{
    $stream = fopen('php://temp', 'r+b');
    fwrite($stream, $bytes);
    rewind($stream);

    return $stream;
}

it('commitStaged keeps the temp file reusable across an outer SQL rollback', function (): void {
    $content = app(ContentStore::class);
    $staged = $content->stage(stringStream('reusable staged bytes'));
    $scope = BlobScope::drive('staged-test');
    try {
        expect(fn () => app(VaulterDatabase::class)->transaction(function () use ($staged, $scope): void {
            app(BlobStore::class)->commitStaged($staged, $scope);
            throw new RuntimeException('metadata rollback');
        }))->toThrow(RuntimeException::class, 'metadata rollback');
        expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(0)
            ->and(is_file($staged->path()))->toBeTrue();
        $blob = app(BlobStore::class)->commitStaged($staged, $scope);
        expect(is_file($staged->path()))->toBeTrue()
            ->and(stream_get_contents(app(BlobStore::class)->read($blob)))->toBe('reusable staged bytes');
        expect(app(BlobStore::class)->commitStaged($staged, $scope)->id)->toBe($blob->id);
    } finally {
        $staged->discard();
    }
    expect(is_file($staged->path()))->toBeFalse();
});

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/BlobCommitTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

/**
 * The content-addressed commit flow (H.3): stage → hash the staged file →
 * find-or-create by the per-scope dedup key → promote bytes only on a newly
 * created row. Server-recompute beats any client claim; dedup yields ONE row and
 * ONE physical object for identical bytes.
 */
beforeEach(function (): void {
    Storage::fake('local');
    config()->set('vaulter.dedup.scope', 'tenant');
});

it('коммитит блоб: одна строка, объект на диске, статус committed', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('hello cas'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect($blob->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed)
        ->and($blob->sha256)->toBe(hash('sha256', 'hello cas'))
        ->and($blob->size_bytes)->toBe(9)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);

    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
});

it('сервер пересчитывает sha256 — врущий клиент игнорируется', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    // The client could send any sha256 they like; commit() never takes it — the
    // hash is recomputed from the staged temp file. We prove it by checking the
    // stored hash matches the REAL bytes, not a lie.
    $blob = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('real bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect($blob->sha256)->toBe(hash('sha256', 'real bytes'))
        ->and($blob->sha256)->not->toBe(hash('sha256', 'a lie'));
});

it('дедуп: одинаковые байты дважды → ОДНА строка, ОДИН объект, два ребра', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $first = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('same bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $second = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('same bytes'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect($second->getKey())->toBe($first->getKey())
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);

    // Two distinct versions reference the one blob — two edges, one physical object.
    $v1 = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(versionNo: 1, name: 'a.bin');
    $v2 = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion(versionNo: 1, name: 'b.bin');
    $store->attach($v1, $first);
    $store->attach($v2, $second);

    expect(Storage::disk('local')->allFiles())->toHaveCount(1);

    // ref_count is NOT bumped synchronously (H.3) — it stays 0 until reconciliation.
    expect($first->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(0);

    // Reconciliation rebuilds ref_count to the edge total (2).
    app(BlobReconciler::class)->reconcile();
    expect($first->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(2);
});

it('повторный коммит идемпотентен и не пишет второй объект', function (): void {
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);

    $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('idem'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));
    $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor('idem'), scope: BlobScope::forUpload((string) config('vaulter.dedup.scope'), (string) 1, 'unused'));

    expect(Storage::disk('local')->allFiles())->toHaveCount(1)
        ->and(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(1);
});

```


# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/Cas/ScopeKeyReconcileTest.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Artisan;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Storage;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\BlobStore;
use Vaulter\Contracts\Cas\ContentStore;
use Vaulter\Database\VaulterDatabase;
use Vaulter\Enums\Cas\BlobStatus;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobGarbageCollector;
use Vaulter\Media\Cas\BlobReconciler;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Cas\CasTestHelpers;
use Vaulter\Media\Tests\Fixtures\Cas\FailingPromoteContentStore;
use Vaulter\Models\Cas\Blob;
use Vaulter\Tests\Concurrency\ForkRace;
use Vaulter\ValueObjects\Cas\BlobScope;

beforeEach(function (): void {
    ForkRace::skipUnlessSupported($this);
    config()->set('vaulter.ids.default', 'ulid');
    $this->artisan('migrate:fresh', ['--force' => true])->assertSuccessful();
    Storage::fake('local');
});

it('collects a post-promote SQL rollback only after grace under the lossless lock', function (): void {
    $content = app(ContentStore::class);
    app()->instance(ContentStore::class, new FailingPromoteContentStore($content, after: true));
    $scope = BlobScope::tenant('019a0000-1234-7000-8000-123456789abc');
    $bytes = 'promoted then rollback';
    expect(fn () => app(BlobStore::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), $scope))->toThrow(RuntimeException::class);
    expect(Blob::query()->count())->toBe(0);
    app()->instance(ContentStore::class, $content);
    $key = $content->keyFor($scope, hash('sha256', $bytes));
    expect(app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans('local', 24, true)['pruned'])->toBe(0);
    CasTestHelpers::ageObject($key, 48);
    $queries = [];
    app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection()->listen(function ($query) use (&$queries): void {
        $queries[] = $query->bindings;
    });
    expect(app(BlobReconciler::class)->sweepOrphans('local', 24, true)['pruned'])->toBe(1);
    expect($queries)->toContain(['cas:'.$scope->key().':'.hash('sha256', $bytes)]);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertMissing($key);
});

it('serializes GC against a writer of the same scope and keeps reachable bytes', function (): void {
    $scope = BlobScope::drive('01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV');
    $bytes = 'race bytes';
    $blob = app(BlobStore::class)->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), $scope);
    $blob->forceFill(['status' => BlobStatus::Tombstoned, 'delete_after_at' => now()->subDay()])->save();
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $outcomes = ForkRace::run([
        fn (): array => app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->sweep(),
        function () use ($scope, $bytes, $version): string {
            return app(VaulterDatabase::class)->transaction(function () use ($scope, $bytes, $version): string {
                $store = app(BlobStore::class);
                $fresh = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), $scope);
                $store->attach($version, $fresh);

                return (string) $fresh->getKey();
            });
        },
    ]);
    foreach ($outcomes as $outcome) {
        expect($outcome->ok)->toBeTrue($outcome->message ?? '');
    }
    expect($blob->fresh()->status)->toBe(BlobStatus::Committed);
    Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($blob->storage_key);
    expect(stream_get_contents(app(BlobStore::class)->read($blob)))->toBe($bytes);
});

it('repairs while batch GC holds the first advisory lock without a lock-order cycle', function (): void {
    $version = CasTestHelpers::makeVersion();
    $scope = BlobScope::drive((string) $version->file->node->storage->getKey());
    $database = app(VaulterDatabase::class);
    // Select a pair where lexical scope order and PostgreSQL lock order disagree.
    for ($i = 0; $i < 100; $i++) {
        $bytes = 'repair GC lock order '.$i;
        $sha = hash('sha256', $bytes);
        $sourceKey = 'cas:legacy:snull:'.$sha;
        $targetKey = 'cas:'.$scope->key().':'.$sha;
        $hashes = $database->connection()->selectOne('SELECT hashtext(?) AS source, hashtext(?) AS target', [$sourceKey, $targetKey]);
        if ($hashes->source < $hashes->target) {
            break;
        }
    }
    expect($hashes->source)->toBeLessThan($hashes->target);
    $store = app(BlobStore::class);
    $source = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), BlobScope::legacy('snull'));
    $target = $store->commit(CasTestHelpers::streamFor($bytes), $scope);
    $version->forceFill(['blob_id' => $source->getKey()])->save();
    $store->attach($version, $source);
    $barrier = sys_get_temp_dir().'/vlt_repair_gc_'.bin2hex(random_bytes(8));
    mkdir($barrier);
    $wait = static function (Closure $condition): void {
        $deadline = microtime(true) + 5;
        while (!$condition()) {
            if (microtime(true) > $deadline) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Repair/GC barrier timed out.');
            }
            usleep(1_000);
        }
    };
    try {
        $outcomes = ForkRace::run([
            function () use ($sourceKey, $barrier, $wait): array {
                $db = app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection();

                // No automatic retry: a deadlock must fail this regression.
                return $db->transaction(function () use ($db, $sourceKey, $barrier, $wait): array {
                    $db->select('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(?))', [$sourceKey]);
                    file_put_contents($barrier.'/held', '1');
                    $wait(static fn (): bool => is_file($barrier.'/repair-pid'));
                    $pid = (int) file_get_contents($barrier.'/repair-pid');
                    $wait(static function () use ($db, $pid): bool {
                        $db->select('SELECT pg_stat_clear_snapshot()');

                        return $db->table('pg_stat_activity')->where('pid', $pid)->where('wait_event', 'advisory')->exists();
                    });

                    return app(BlobGarbageCollector::class)->mark(24);
                }, 1);
            },
            function () use ($barrier, $wait, $targetKey): array {
                $wait(static fn (): bool => is_file($barrier.'/held'));
                $db = app(VaulterDatabase::class)->connection();
                $pid = (int) $db->selectOne('SELECT pg_backend_pid() AS pid')->pid;
                $attempts = 0;
                $db->beforeExecuting(static function (string $query, array $bindings) use ($barrier, $pid, $targetKey, &$attempts): void {
                    if (str_contains($query, 'pg_advisory_xact_lock') && str_contains(json_encode($bindings, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR), 'cas:')) {
                        file_put_contents($barrier.'/repair-pid', (string) $pid);
                        if (str_contains(json_encode($bindings, JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR), $targetKey)) {
                            $attempts++;
                        }
                    }
                });

                $exit = Artisan::call('vaulter:blobs:repair-scopes', ['--execute' => true]);

                return ['exit' => $exit, 'attempts' => $attempts];
            },
        ]);
        foreach ($outcomes as $outcome) {
            expect($outcome->ok)->toBeTrue($outcome->message ?? '');
        }
        expect($outcomes[1]->value)->toBe(['exit' => 0, 'attempts' => 1])
            ->and($version->fresh()->blob_id)->toBe($target->getKey())
            ->and($target->fresh()->ref_count)->toBe(1)
            ->and(stream_get_contents($store->read($target)))->toBe($bytes);
        Storage::disk('local')->assertExists($source->storage_key);
    } finally {
        foreach (glob($barrier.'/*') ?: [] as $file) {
            unlink($file);
        }
        rmdir($barrier);
    }
});

# COMPLETE FILE packages/media/tests/TestCase.php
```
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Vaulter\Media\Tests;

use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Orchestra\Testbench\TestCase as OrchestraTestCase;
use Vaulter\Media\VaulterMediaServiceProvider;
use Vaulter\VaulterServiceProvider;

abstract class TestCase extends OrchestraTestCase
{
    /**
     * @param  Application              $app
     * @return array<int, class-string>
     */
    protected function getPackageProviders($app): array
    {
        return [
            // Core provides the Contracts\Cas seam, the Blob model + v_blobs schema
            // and Support\Config; this package binds the concrete CAS engine on top.
            VaulterServiceProvider::class,
            VaulterMediaServiceProvider::class,
        ];
    }

    /**
     * @param Application $app
     */
    protected function defineEnvironment($app): void
    {
        $app['config']->set('database.default', 'testing');
        $app['config']->set('database.connections.testing', $this->suiteDatabaseConnection());

        // The CAS slice is ACL-agnostic: run OPEN under the env-gated null driver so
        // the tests exercise pure blob mechanics without an access-control lane.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.drivers.permission', 'null');

        // Pin the auto-increment strategy to bigint — the CAS tests assert integer
        // keys on the Storage→Node→File→Version chain they build.
        $app['config']->set('vaulter.ids.default', 'bigint');
    }

    protected function defineDatabaseMigrations(): void
    {
        // The v_blobs (+ Drive) schema is owned by core; load its migrations through
        // the path-repo symlink that Composer places in this package's vendor dir.
        $this->loadMigrationsFrom(__DIR__.'/../vendor/axioma-studio/vaulter-core/database/migrations');
    }

    /**
     * The connection the whole suite runs on. Default: in-memory SQLite. Opt INTO
     * Postgres by exporting DB_CONNECTION=pgsql (plus the DB_* coordinates): the
     * dedicated pgsql CI lane exercises the real advisory-xact-lock / FOR UPDATE /
     * SERIALIZABLE critical sections that SQLite cannot model — every such test
     * SKIPS unless the live driver is pgsql (master plan P4 #11).
     *
     * @return array<string, string|bool|null>
     */
    protected function suiteDatabaseConnection(): array
    {
        if (env('DB_CONNECTION') !== 'pgsql') {
            return [
                'driver' => 'sqlite',
                'database' => ':memory:',
                'prefix' => '',
            ];
        }

        return [
            'driver' => 'pgsql',
            'host' => (string) env('DB_HOST', '127.0.0.1'),
            'port' => (string) env('DB_PORT', '5432'),
            'database' => (string) env('DB_DATABASE', 'vaulter_test'),
            'username' => (string) env('DB_USERNAME', 'vaulter'),
            'password' => (string) env('DB_PASSWORD', ''),
            'charset' => 'utf8',
            'prefix' => '',
            'prefix_indexes' => true,
            'search_path' => 'public',
            'sslmode' => 'prefer',
        ];
    }
}
